# v4.1 — "The skill sheds the ritual" Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Rewire the canonical `second-opinion` skill to delegate Stages 1–3 (and Stage-5 artifact materialization) to the v4.0 `amicus council run` engine — keeping the human Stages 0/4/6 Claude-orchestrated — and add headless **debate mode** (Stage 2.5) to the engine so the daily council collapses from ~30 hand-driven tool calls to one engine call plus three human stages.

**Architecture:** Two separable features over the v4.0 engine. **Feature 2 (debate mode, spec §5)** adds a Stage-2.5 rebuttal round inside the engine: a provisional no-ledger tally identifies Contested/Disputed findings → one defense mini-wave (raiser solos) → one re-vote mini-wave (disputing judges) → a final ledgered tally, all as new `run.json` stage checkpoints, with strict trailing-JSON briefing/parse contracts mirroring the v4.0 Stage-2 style and a NEW pure module `src/council/debate.js` for final-tally reassembly. **Feature 1 (skill fast path, spec §4)** rewrites `skills/second-opinion/SKILL.md` to author only the briefing (Stage 0), invoke `amicus council run` once (Stages 1–3+5-artifacts), then run the human decision/lessons stages over the engine's `tally.json`/`run.json`; the old manual mechanics move verbatim-plus-headnote to a new `MANUAL-ORCHESTRATION.md` fallback; a `--claude-review` engine input keeps the Claude-in-council toggle usable in the fast path; a `--render` flag on `council verdict` refreshes the decided `report.html` in place.

**Tech Stack:** Node ≥ 22.12 CommonJS, jest 29, ajv (devDependency, landed by v4.0 Plan A) for schema tests, zod (MCP input schemas — existing). Zero new runtime dependencies, zero new devDependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-v4.1-skill-on-engine-design.md` — covers §4 (skill fast path: §4.2 stage map, §4.3 briefing/date-stamp, §4.4 Claude-in-council, §4.5 CLI/MCP deltas, §4.6 degradation, §4.7 fallback/preflight, §4.8 file moves/postinstall, §4.9 docs-drift), §5 (debate mode: §5.1 state machine, §5.2 CLI/MCP, §5.3 briefings, §5.4 parsers, §5.5 reassembly, §5.6 withdrawals, §5.7 degradation), §6 (testing), §7 (rollout), §8 (compatibility), and all "Resolved questions (Christian, 2026-07-19)" OQ-1…OQ-7 (adopted as written).

**PLAN BOUNDARY (this rev vs siblings):** Debate mode (spec §5) lives **HERE**, not v4.2 — the v4.0 spec deferred it to "v4.1 (or v4.2 at the latest)" and this rev claims it. The `--debate` CLI flag + the `debate?` MCP input + the `run.json` `debate` summary object are a **durable public contract**: v4.5 will make `debate` a pack-able policy option, so their names/shapes must not churn after this lands. This rev does NOT build: policy packs / run profiles (v4.5), local providers (v4.2), watch/streaming/Council Workspace GUI (v4.3/v4.4), `--dry-run` cost preview (backlog), parallel panels, interactive/GUI debate. It adds **zero** new MCP tools (only new inputs on `amicus_council_run` and `amicus_verdict`); tool count stays 15. Feature 1 (skill, Phases 2–4) and Feature 2 (debate engine, Phase 1) are **separable** — Phase 1 ships value with no skill change — but both ride the single **v4.1.0** point release (Phase 6).

## Ambiguities & drift resolved while planning (visible decisions)

1. **Debate wave orchestration lives in a NEW `src/council/run-debate.js`, separate from the pure `src/council/debate.js`.** Spec §5.5 mandates `debate.js` as the *pure* reassembly module (DI-free); but the impure wave orchestration (launch defense solos, launch the re-vote fanout, parse, checkpoint) needs the DI launchers and would push `run.js` (Plan B driver, already dense — Plan B Task 10) past the 300-line gate. So the orchestration goes in `run-debate.js` (`runDebate(ctx, {...})`, launcher-injected like `run-stages.js`), and `debate.js` stays pure. Both are new files < 300 lines. Even with the orchestration out of the driver the merged `run.js` is 277 lines, so **Task 0.5** first extracts the chair block into `src/council/run-chair.js` (leaving ~195) before Task 4's ~45-line insert — the 300-line gate is never relaxed and `run.js` is never grandfathered. (Global Constraints; spec §5.1/§5.5.)
2. **Debate briefing templates live in a NEW `src/council/briefings-debate.js`, not appended to `briefings-stage2.js`.** `briefings-stage2.js` is a v4.0-new file already carrying judge bundle + chair packet + repairs; adding three debate templates risks the 300-line gate. New file keeps both under gate and keeps the debate contract isolated (the durable-public-contract concern above). (Spec §5.3.)
3. **Parser action verb vs finding decoration verb.** `parseDebateDefense` (spec §5.4) returns present-tense actions `defend|amend|withdraw|no-response`; the finding decoration (spec §5.6) uses past-tense `defended|amended|withdrawn|no-response`. `debate.js` owns the single mapping (`defend→defended`, `amend→amended`, `withdraw→withdrawn`, `no-response→no-response`) so no other module re-derives it.
4. **Date-stamp move touches v4.0-new `briefings-stage2.js`.** Spec §4.3 says `briefings.js` prepends `Today's date is YYYY-MM-DD.` to *every* composed model-facing briefing. In v4.0, `briefings.js`'s `compose()` already injects the date into Stage-1 seat/critic/lens briefings (Plan B Task 3 `dateLine`), but the Stage-2 judge bundle and chair packet (`briefings-stage2.js`) do **not**. So the v4.1 date-stamp addition = add an optional `date` to `buildJudgeBundle`/`buildChairPacket` (and every debate template) that prepends the same `dateLine`. Additive/optional so v4.0 call sites without a date still compose. The ONLY `buildJudgeBundle` call site in the whole codebase is `src/council/run-stages.js:149` (inside `runStage2`) — **Task 2** updates it to pass `o.date`, otherwise the Stage-2 judge bundle is never date-stamped and §4.3 fails silently; the `buildChairPacket` call site stays in `run.js` — Task 0.5 moves only the chair chain, see its "Not moved" list — and is updated in Task 4. (Spec §4.3; anchor `briefings-stage2.js` is V4.0-NEW.)
5. **ERROR_CODES spelling.** Spec §4.4 spells the pre-flight code `council_claude_review_invalid`; the repo `ERROR_CODES` object convention is SCREAMING_SNAKE (v4.0 Plan B Task 11 established `COUNCIL_QUORUM`/`COST_EXCEEDED`). This plan follows the repo convention: `ERROR_CODES.COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID = 'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID'`; the human/error-doc `message` carries the spec's lowercase token verbatim for grep-ability.
6. **`--render` appends after the pinned verdict-command tail (spec §4.9 B22).** The B22 docs-drift pin matches `amicus council verdict … --decisions … -o …`; §4.5c requires `--render` to be documented *after* that tail so the pin's regex still matches. In `src/cli.js` usage and SKILL.md, `--render` is added as the last flag line of the `council verdict` block; `isBooleanFlag` gains `render`.
7. **`--no-cost-gate` is already a registered boolean flag** (`src/cli.js:136`) — v4.1 does not re-register it; the CLI handler for `council run` (v4.0-new `src/cli-handlers-council-run.js`) forwards `args['no-cost-gate']` into `runCouncil` options as `noCostGate`, and `run-launch.js`'s `launchWave` passes a per-call `noCostGate` into `runFanout` (verified key: `src/sidecar/fanout.js` guards the budget gate with `if (!options.noCostGate)`), so every module that ASSEMBLES launch options must include it — `run-stages.js` (Stage-1 `common`, the findings-repair solo, the Stage-2 judge wave, the judge-repair solo), `run-chair.js` (Task 0.5), and `run-debate.js` (Task 4). `--debate` and `--render` ARE new boolean flags added to `isBooleanFlag`. (Spec §4.5b/§4.5d.)
8. **Claude review is review N+1 sourced from a file — it never spawns a leg.** `--claude-review <path>` is validated pre-flight via the existing `validateFindings`; on success its findings enter `toGlobalFindings` as one more labeled entry and its runStats row is synthesized `{model:'claude', role:'claude', status:'complete', durationMs:null, usage:null, conformance:'clean'}` (nulls per the never-invent rule). No fanout/solo leg is launched for `claude`; no judge leg either — enforced by keeping the judge ROSTER (`run-stages.js:152` `judges = reviews.map(r => r.modelInput)`) decoupled from the judge BUNDLE's labeled-review list, which Task 7 widens with an `extraLabeled` argument. `claude` is likewise excluded from `pickFallbackChair` so a `claude` ledger row can never be promoted to chair on a later run. (Spec §4.4a; OQ-2.)
9. **`--chair claude` guard is literal on the reserved seat name.** Any alias resolving to an Anthropic model as chair is out of scope; the enforced guard is the literal string `claude` as `--chair` value (pre-flight error, same envelope as chair-in-bench). (Spec §4.4a.)
10. **Debate decoration is injected into the tally `record` before `writeTallyFiles`, and carried through `buildVerdict`.** `buildVerdict` (Plan A gave it `(record, decisions, opts)`) maps `record.findings[].debate` straight onto the verdict finding when present — additive; council family stays schemaVersion 2. (Spec §5.6; anchor `verdict.js` — the v4.1 change is that `buildVerdict` copies the additive `debate` field through.)
11. **The skill never re-runs `council tally`.** The engine's `tally-final` already appended the ledger row(s); SKILL.md's fast path reads conformance/runStats from `run.json`/`tally.json` and NEVER invokes `council tally` (the double-append guard, pinned by the new `skill-fast-path-docs.test.js`). (Spec §4.2 Stage-6 note; §4.9.)
12. **Fast-path artifacts retire `crossreview-matrix.md` + `verdict.md` (OQ-4).** In the fast path the engine's `report.html`/`report.md --md` section supersedes `crossreview-matrix.md` and `chair-output.md` is the chair-prose artifact (`verdict.md`'s role). The manual path keeps the old names. No copy-to-old-name shim is shipped (OQ-4 final: "I'd rather not ship two names for one artifact").
13. **Stage-0 menu shrinks to four items (OQ-5).** Chair verdict scale is standard-on in the fast path (the engine mandates the `VERDICT:` line headless — v4.0 locked decision 1); the menu notes it "now standard" rather than offering it. The manual path keeps the 5-item menu.
14. **Red-team folds into `--critic` (OQ-3).** Fast-path red-team requests map to `--critic <model>`; fully custom per-seat briefs are a documented manual-path case. The skill drops the standalone red-team-variant launch block from SKILL.md (it moves to MANUAL-ORCHESTRATION.md).
15. **`amicus_verdict render:true` requires an explicit `outDir` to write.** The MCP tool is pure/does-not-write today; §4.5c adds a write side-effect *only when* `render:true` AND `outDir` (absolute) is given — otherwise the tool returns the `--md` rendering in the result but writes nothing (Cowork then writes the file with host tools). This keeps the "pure by default" posture and avoids guessing a write location. (Spec §4.5c; anchor `mcp-server.js:1144`.)
16. **Version is v4.1.0 minor (OQ-7).** Every engine change is additive (flags, MCP inputs, additive JSON fields); the SKILL.md overwrite-on-update is existing postinstall policy; MODEL-NOTES stays machine-local `if-missing`. This rev is its own point release.

## Prerequisites & re-grounding

### Hard ordering (must be MERGED to main before Task 0)

- **v4.0 (headless council engine) — HARD DEP for everything.** This rev consumes the v4.0 engine end to end: `amicus council run` CLI, `run.json`/`tally.json`/`verdict.json` shapes, the `amicus_council_run` MCP tool, the `{schemaVersion,type}` envelope, JSON-tool prompt-injection fencing, `stripFoldMarkers`/fold-nonce, and `sanitizeName`. Debate mode extends the v4.0 state machine *in place*; the skill fast path *drives* the v4.0 CLI. If `src/council/run.js` is absent at Task 0, **STOP — v4.0 is not merged.**
- No other sibling rev is required. v4.1 is additive over v4.0 and does not depend on v4.2/v4.3/v4.4.

## Global Constraints

- **Baseline for execution:** then-current main with **v4.0 merged** (Task 0 verifies). Authoritative spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-v4.1-skill-on-engine-design.md` (finalized; every "Resolved questions (Christian, 2026-07-19)" decision LOCKED). Consumed contracts: the v4.0 spec + Plan A/B (cited above).
- **Version floors:** Node ≥ 22.12.0, CommonJS. New files start with `'use strict';`.
- **Additive only (spec §8):** the council doc family stays **schemaVersion 2**; every new JSON field (`run.json` `debate`, tally/verdict `findings[].debate`, `meta.claudeInCouncil:true`) is additive-injection — no re-nesting, existing key-readers keep working. MCP tool count stays **15** (only new inputs on `amicus_council_run`/`amicus_verdict`).
- **Exactly-one debate round is structural (spec §5.7):** the state machine has no edge back into a debate stage — never add one.
- **Durable public contract:** the `--debate` flag name, the MCP `debate?` boolean input, and the `run.json` `debate` summary object shape are frozen after this rev (v4.5 packs them). Do not rename or re-nest them.
- **300-line gate (NOT relaxed):** `node scripts/check-file-sizes.js --all` passes after every task (it prints nothing on success — silence + exit 0 is the pass signal). Every NEW file (`run-chair.js`, `debate.js`, `run-debate.js`, `briefings-debate.js`) stays < 300 lines. `src/mcp-server.js`, `src/mcp-tools.js`, `src/cli.js` are grandfathered (may be edited, must NOT be extracted here). **`src/council/run.js` is NOT grandfathered** — `scripts/check-file-sizes.js` sets `maxLines: 300`, includes `src/**/*.js`, and its `exclude` list contains no `src/council/*` path; at 277 lines it is why **Task 0.5** extracts `run-chair.js` before Tasks 4 and 7. v4.0-new files edited here must stay under gate.
- **Zero new runtime deps, zero new devDeps:** `dependencies` and `devDependencies` in `package.json` unchanged (`ajv` devDep already landed by v4.0 Plan A).
- **Lint clean:** `npm run lint` (eslint on `src/`) passes after every task. Style: `'use strict'`, 2-space indent, single quotes, semicolons, trailing newline, curly braces on one-line ifs.
- **Suite green is the gate:** `npx jest` (default suite) green after every task. The default suite **excludes** `*.integration.test.js` (jest.config.js); run targeted with `npm test -- tests/<file>.test.js`. Suite baseline (measured on merged v4.0.1) = **324 suites / 4067 passed / 5 skipped / 0 failed** — verified in Task 0 Step 5; every task must end at 0 failures with counts >= this baseline. A task never ends on a red suite: where a later task's edits would turn another suite red, restructure the step order so the suite is green at each commit.

- **Commits:** conventional commits, one per task (or per step where marked); every commit message ends with a `Co-Authored-By:` trailer naming **the model that actually authored the commit** (e.g. `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`) — not a hardcoded model name.

Never push without Christian's explicit go (the release task is USER-GATED).
- **Windows is the reference platform:** all paths via `path.join`/`path.resolve`; file writes use `writeFileAtomic` for state, `fs.writeFileSync(..., { mode: 0o600 })` for artifacts; tests use `os.tmpdir()` scratch dirs and never rely on symlink privileges.

Every doc-suite change cites its §4.9 row. ## Shared contracts (referenced by every task)

**CLI/MCP surface deltas (spec §4.5, §5.2):**

```
amicus council run … [--debate] [--claude-review <path>] [--no-cost-gate]
amicus council verdict <tally.json> [--decisions <d.json>] [-o <out.json>] [--render]
amicus_council_run { …, debate?: boolean, claudeReviewFile?: string, noCostGate?: boolean }
amicus_verdict { record, decisions?, render?: boolean, outDir?: string, project? }
```

**`runCouncil` options gain (Tasks 4/7/8):** `debate?: boolean` (default false), `claudeReviewFile?: string|null`, `noCostGate?: boolean` (default false). All additive; a v4.0 caller omitting them behaves exactly as before.

**Debate parser results (spec §5.4, Task 1):**

```
parseDebateDefense(text, expectedIds) → {
  byId: { [id]: { action: 'defend'|'amend'|'withdraw', argument?: string, claim?: string }
                | { action: 'no-response' } },   // every expectedId present
  ok: boolean,                                    // false ⇒ caller issues 1 repair
  errors: [{ code, detail }]                      // NO_FENCED_BLOCK, NOT_PARSEABLE, BAD_RESPONSES
}
parseRevote(text, expectedIds) → {
  byId: { [id]: { verdict: 'agree'|'dispute'|'neutral', reason?: string } },  // only present-and-valid ids
  ok: boolean,
  errors: [{ code, detail }]                      // NO_FENCED_BLOCK, NOT_PARSEABLE, BAD_REVOTES
}
```
Whole-block failure semantics: the caller (run-debate) issues exactly **1** repair re-prompt (solo, quoting `errors`); still-bad ⇒ for defense every expected id becomes `{action:'no-response'}`, for re-vote every id is dropped (originals stand); the leg's `conformance` is recorded `'unstructured'`. Unknown ids not in `expectedIds` are ignored. A *present-but-individually-invalid* id (amend without claim; bad verdict enum) becomes `no-response` for defense / dropped for re-vote — no repair for a well-formed block with one bad entry (`ok:true`, per-id fallback).

**`debate.json` artifact (spec §5.4):**

```jsonc
{
  "findings": [ { "id": "A1", "raiser": "gemini", "action": "defend"|"amend"|"withdraw"|"no-response",
                  "argument": "…"?, "claim": "…"?, "previousTier": "Contested" } ],
  "revotes":  [ { "judge": "gpt", "id": "A1", "verdict": "agree"|"dispute"|"neutral", "reason": "…"?, "applied": true } ]
}
```

**`run.json` top-level `debate` summary (spec §5.1, DURABLE PUBLIC CONTRACT):**

```jsonc
"debate": {
  "enabled": true,
  "outcome": "ran" | "nothing-to-debate" | "skipped-cost-ceiling",
  "contested": 2, "disputed": 1,
  "defended": 1, "amended": 1, "withdrawn": 1, "noResponse": 0,
  "revoteJudges": 2, "revoteApplied": 3, "verdictChanges": 1
}
```
`run.json.stages[]` gains entries named `tally-provisional`, `debate-defense`, `debate-revote`, `tally-final` with the standard `{name, status, startedAt, completedAt, taskIds?|waveId?}` shape. Without `--debate` the v4.0 stage sequence is byte-unchanged (no `debate` key at all — the key is spread into `initRun` only when `o.debate` is true, **never** written as `null`; `tally` stage name kept). Whenever the key IS present it always carries a valid `outcome`, including when a run aborts mid-debate: Task 4's `initRun` seed writes `{enabled: true, outcome: 'nothing-to-debate'}` on the first write and only ever overwrites it with another valid outcome.

> **Stage-order divergence from spec §5.1 — deliberate, do NOT "fix" it.** §5.1's diagram draws `… debate-revote → tally-final → chair → verdict`; this plan keeps shipped v4.0's real order, `tally-provisional → debate-defense → debate-revote → chair → tally-final → verdict`. Merged v4.0.1 `run.js` runs the chair at `:181-252` and the final `tally()` from `:254`, and it must: the final tally input is `mkInput(chairStats, actualChair || o.chair)`, so the chair's own runStats row **and** any fallback-promoted chair name are INPUTS to the final tally — running the chair after it would drop both. Everything §5.1 actually constrains is preserved: the provisional tally never ledgers, the chair packet is built from the (post-debate) provisional record, and the ledgered final tally is what `tally.json`/`verdict.json`/`report.html` consume.

**Finding decoration (spec §5.6, additive on tally.json + verdict.json findings):**

```jsonc
"debate": { "action": "defended"|"amended"|"withdrawn"|"no-response", "previousTier": "Contested" }
```
Present only on findings that entered the debate (Contested/Disputed in the provisional tally). Absent otherwise. `debate.js` maps the parser's present-tense action → this past-tense action.

**Claude-review contract (spec §4.4, Task 7):** `--claude-review <path>` / `claudeReviewFile`. The file is a normal Stage-1 review (prose + trailing findings JSON). Pre-flight: `validateFindings(fileText)` invalid ⇒ error doc `{code:'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID', message:'council_claude_review_invalid: <detail>'}`, exit 1, **zero launches**. Valid ⇒ its findings become label N+1 via `toGlobalFindings`; `meta.claudeInCouncil = true`; `'claude'` appended to `meta.models`; `runStats` gains `{model:'claude', role:'claude', wasChair:false, conformance:'clean', status:'complete', durationMs:null, usage:null}`; no leg launched; no judge leg for claude; `--chair claude` is a pre-flight error (same envelope as chair-in-bench). The judge bundle carries claude via `runStage2(ctx, {…, extraLabeled})` (`src/council/run-stages.js`) while the judge roster stays bench-only; `pickFallbackChair` (`src/council/run-chair.js` after Task 0.5, still re-exported from `./run`) also excludes the literal `'claude'`, so the ledger row this creates can never be promoted to chair on a later run.

**`--render` contract (spec §4.5c, Task 9):** CLI `council verdict … -o <out.json> --render` writes `verdict.json` (as today) then `buildReport({verdict}, {format:'html'})` → writes `<dir(out)>/report.html`. MCP `amicus_verdict {render:true, outDir:'<abs>'}` returns `buildReport(...,{format:'md'})` in the fenced tool result AND writes `<outDir>/report.html`; `outDir` is resolved against the project dir and rejected with an `isError` result if it escapes it (`isPathInside`, the same guard `amicus_council_run` applies), and `render:true` without `outDir` returns the md rendering but writes nothing.

**Test helper (extends v4.0 `tests/council/helpers/fake-launchers.js`):** debate tests add scripted defense/re-vote outputs keyed by stage/wave id. A defense solo output = `<prose>\n\`\`\`json\n{"responses":[…]}\n\`\`\`\n`; a re-vote leg output = `<prose>\n\`\`\`json\n{"revotes":[…]}\n\`\`\`\n`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Pin the v4.0 `run.js` + `run-stages.js` driver flow (the debate-insertion seam)**

Read the merged `src/council/run.js` and record where the provisional tally is computed and where the chair packet is built (Plan B Task 10 put both between Stage-2 and the chair). `run.js` delegates both stages to `src/council/run-stages.js`, so pin that file too — it owns the **only** `buildJudgeBundle` call site in the codebase (Task 7's `--claude-review` seam) and the per-seat lens wave-id scheme (Task 4 Step 7's test). The debate insertion (Task 4) goes: after `runStage2`, compute the provisional tally, and if `--debate` is on, run `run-debate` before building the chair packet:

```bash
grep -n "provisional\|buildChairPacket\|mkInput\|runStage2\|updateStage\|appendStageWave\|appendRunFn\|writeTallyFiles\|writeVerdictFiles" src/council/run.js
grep -n "buildJudgeBundle\|modelInput\|appendStageWave\|runId}-l\|runId}-s" src/council/run-stages.js
```

### Task 0.5: Extract chair synthesis into `run-chair.js` (line-budget prerequisite)

`src/council/run.js` is **277 lines** on merged v4.0.1 and is fully size-gated (`scripts/check-file-sizes.js` `maxLines: 300`, `include: ['src/**/*.js', …]`, and **no** `src/council/*` entry in its `exclude` list). Tasks 4 and 7 add ~45 and ~25 lines to that file, which would blow the gate. This task moves the chair block out FIRST so the later tasks have real margin. It is a **pure refactor — zero behavior change** — so its TDD shape is CHARACTERIZATION: pin the observable behavior, watch the pin pass against the untouched driver, move the code, watch the same pin pass again. Runs immediately after Task 0; it **must** land before Task 4.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/council/run-chair.js` (~135 lines — chair chain + VERDICT-line repair, launcher-injected)
- Modify: `src/council/run.js` (V4.0-NEW — delete the chair block + `pickFallbackChair`; call `runChair`; re-export `pickFallbackChair`; **277 → ~195 lines**)
- Test: `tests/council/run-chair-seam.test.js` (Create — characterization pins for the seam)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `stage2.buildChairRepairPrompt` (`briefings-stage2.js`, C5); `parseChairVerdict` (`parse-stage2.js`, C4); `runState.appendStageWave`/`updateStage`/`checkpoint` (C8); `isAbortExit` (`run-stages.js`); `ctx.launchers.launchSolo` (C6). Nothing new — every require moves with the code it serves.
- Produces (consumed by `run.js` only):
  - `runChair(ctx, { packet, degraded, statsFn, isSignalled }) → Promise<{aborted, chairLeg, actualChair, chairText, chairConformance, overallVerdict}>` — the whole `chair` stage: budget skip → attempt `-ch1` → retry `-ch2` → ledger-promoted fallback `-ch3` → give up, then the single VERDICT-line repair `-ch4`. `ctx` is run.js's existing `{o, launchers, addWave, overBudget, scratchDir}` (the same object `runStage1`/`runStage2` already take). `degraded` is run.js's `{value:boolean}` box, mutated in place exactly as before. **`aborted: number|null`** — the extracted code cannot call `finalize()`, so every `return finalize(exitCode || signalled)` becomes `return bail(code)`; run.js finalizes on `aborted !== null`, writing NO tally and NO verdict (identical to v4.0).
  - `pickFallbackChair(statsRows, bench, failedChair)` — moved verbatim; **`run.js` re-exports it**, so `tests/council/run-chair.test.js:6` (`require('../../src/council/run')`) keeps working and `module.exports` in run.js stays byte-identical.
- **`isSignalled` is a getter, not a value.** run.js's signal handler mutates its `signalled` local between awaits, and the v4.0 code re-reads it after every chair launch. run.js passes `isSignalled: () => signalled`; passing the snapshot would silently drop mid-chair aborts. This is the single most fragile part of the move — Step 1 pins it twice.
- Not moved (Task 4 edits these in place, so they must stay in `run.js`): `mkInput`, the provisional `tally()`, `stage2.buildChairPacket(...)`, the `chair-packet.md` write, and everything from `// ---- Final tally` down. Task 4 Step 5's edit anchors are therefore unaffected by this task.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the characterization test**

Create `tests/council/run-chair-seam.test.js`. It pins the seam invariants that the existing chair suites do NOT cover — the module's export surface, packet threading, `-ch4` sub-wave registration, the `degraded` box crossing the return boundary, and (twice) the mid-launch signal re-read:

```js
// tests/council/run-chair-seam.test.js
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const runMod = require('../../src/council/run');
const { runCouncil } = runMod;
const runState = require('../../src/council/run-state');
const { scriptedLaunchers, happyScript, baseOptions, mkLeg, okWave } =
  require('./helpers/fake-launchers');

let tmp;
beforeEach(() => { tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'council-chair-seam-')); });
afterEach(() => { fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

const noSignals = () => () => {};
const runDir = () => path.join(tmp, 'council-abc123');
const deps = (launchers, extra = {}) => ({
  launchers, appendRunFn: jest.fn(), statsFn: () => [],
  installSignalAbortFn: noSignals, ...extra,
});
const chairStage = (run) => ((run && run.stages) || []).find(s => s.name === 'chair') || null;
const readInput = () => JSON.parse(
  fs.readFileSync(path.join(runDir(), 'tally-input.json'), 'utf-8'));

describe('run.js export surface (must survive the extraction)', () => {
  test('runCouncil, pickFallbackChair and SIGNAL_EXIT stay exported from ./run', () => {
    expect(typeof runMod.runCouncil).toBe('function');
    expect(typeof runMod.pickFallbackChair).toBe('function');
    expect(runMod.SIGNAL_EXIT).toEqual({ SIGINT: 130, SIGTERM: 143, SIGBREAK: 143 });
  });
});

describe('chair packet threading + artifacts', () => {
  test('the -ch1 prompt is byte-identical to chair-packet.md; prose + chair name reach the artifacts', async () => {
    const script = happyScript();
    let ch1Prompt = null;
    script['abc123-ch1'] = (opts) => {
      ch1Prompt = opts.prompt;
      return okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Synthesis of the bench.\n\nVERDICT: Ship it', 'complete', 0.03)]);
    };
    const { exitCode } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp), deps(scriptedLaunchers(script)));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(ch1Prompt).toBe(fs.readFileSync(path.join(runDir(), 'chair-packet.md'), 'utf-8'));
    expect(fs.readFileSync(path.join(runDir(), 'chair-output.md'), 'utf-8'))
      .toContain('Synthesis of the bench.');
    const verdict = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(runDir(), 'verdict.json'), 'utf-8'));
    expect(verdict.chair).toBe('deepseek');
    expect(verdict.overallVerdict).toBe('Ship it');
    expect(readInput().runStats.find(r => r.wasChair))
      .toMatchObject({ model: 'deepseek', role: 'chair', conformance: 'clean' });
  });

  test('every chair launch carries the run options and targets the run dir (never _scratch)', async () => {
    const script = happyScript();
    script['abc123-ch1'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Prose, no verdict line.', 'complete', 0.03)]);
    script['abc123-ch4'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'VERDICT: Ship it')]);
    const launchers = scriptedLaunchers(script);
    await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp), deps(launchers));

    const chairCalls = launchers.calls.filter(c => /-ch\d$/.test(c.waveId));
    expect(chairCalls.map(c => c.waveId)).toEqual(['abc123-ch1', 'abc123-ch4']);
    for (const c of chairCalls) {
      expect(c.project).toBe(runDir());     // the chair is NOT judge-isolated
      expect(c.timeout).toBe(5);
      expect(c.gateway).toBe('auto');
      expect(c.noValidateModel).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

describe('abort-cascade registration + degraded propagation across the seam', () => {
  test('the VERDICT repair registers -ch4 on the chair stage BEFORE launching', async () => {
    const script = happyScript();
    script['abc123-ch1'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Prose only.', 'complete', 0.03)]);
    let midFlight = null;
    script['abc123-ch4'] = () => {
      midFlight = chairStage(runState.readRun(runDir()));   // observed from inside the launch
      return okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'VERDICT: Ship it')]);
    };
    const { exitCode } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp), deps(scriptedLaunchers(script)));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(midFlight.waveIds).toEqual(['abc123-ch1', 'abc123-ch4']);
    expect(readInput().runStats.find(r => r.wasChair).conformance).toBe('repaired');
  });

  test('a chair give-up degrades to exit 2 but still writes the final tally and ledgers once', async () => {
    const script = happyScript();
    script['abc123-ch1'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', '', 'error')], 1, 'error');
    script['abc123-ch2'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', '', 'error')], 1, 'error');
    const appendRunFn = jest.fn();
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp),
      deps(scriptedLaunchers(script), { appendRunFn }));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);                       // degraded.value crossed the boundary
    expect(chairStage(run).status).toBe('error');
    expect(appendRunFn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(runDir(), 'tally.json'))).toBe(true);
    expect(readInput().runStats.some(r => r.wasChair)).toBe(false);
    expect(runState.readRun(runDir()).chair).toBe('deepseek');  // give-up keeps the requested chair
  });
});

describe('signals are re-read AFTER every chair launch (getter, not snapshot)', () => {
  test('SIGINT landing during the chair solo → exit 130, no tally, no verdict, no ledger row', async () => {
    let onAbort;
    const installSignalAbortFn = (opts) => { onAbort = opts.onAbort; return () => {}; };
    const script = happyScript();
    script['abc123-ch1'] = () => {
      onAbort('SIGINT');                  // lands while the chair leg is in flight
      return okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Synthesis.\nVERDICT: Ship it', 'complete', 0.03)]);
    };
    const appendRunFn = jest.fn();
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp),
      deps(scriptedLaunchers(script), { appendRunFn, installSignalAbortFn }));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(130);
    expect(run.status).toBe('aborted');
    expect(appendRunFn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(runDir(), 'tally.json'))).toBe(false);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(runDir(), 'verdict.json'))).toBe(false);
  });

  test('SIGTERM landing during the -ch4 repair → exit 143, no verdict', async () => {
    let onAbort;
    const installSignalAbortFn = (opts) => { onAbort = opts.onAbort; return () => {}; };
    const script = happyScript();
    script['abc123-ch1'] = () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Prose only.', 'complete', 0.03)]);
    script['abc123-ch4'] = () => {
      onAbort('SIGTERM');
      return okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'VERDICT: Ship it')]);
    };
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp),
      deps(scriptedLaunchers(script), { installSignalAbortFn }));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(143);
    expect(run.status).toBe('aborted');
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(runDir(), 'verdict.json'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('cost ceiling still skips the chair without launching it', () => {
  test('maxCost tripped before the chair: stage skipped, no -ch1, exit 2, verdict written', async () => {
    const launchers = scriptedLaunchers(happyScript());
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp, { maxCost: 0.05 }), deps(launchers));

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(launchers.calls.some(c => c.waveId === 'abc123-ch1')).toBe(false);
    expect(chairStage(run).status).toBe('skipped');
    expect(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(runDir(), 'verdict.json'), 'utf-8')).overallVerdict)
      .toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `src/council/run-chair.js`**

Move `pickFallbackChair` (today `src/council/run.js:35-49`, jsdoc included) and the chair block (today `src/council/run.js:181-252` — from `const attemptChair = async (model, waveId) => {` through `if (!chairLeg || !overallVerdict) { degraded.value = true; }`) into the new file **verbatim**, with only these mechanical substitutions: `signalled` → `isSignalled()`, `return finalize(x)` → `return bail(x)`, `addWave`/`overBudget`/`launchers`/`o` sourced from `ctx`, and a local `now`.

```js
// src/council/run-chair.js
'use strict';

/**
 * @module council/run-chair
 * Chair synthesis + VERDICT-line repair for the headless council driver,
 * lifted VERBATIM out of run.js for the 300-line gate (v4.1 Task 0.5). Pure
 * refactor: same launches, same waveIds, same run.json checkpoints, same
 * degradation rules. Launchers come in through `ctx` exactly as run-stages.js
 * takes them; run.js keeps the chair-packet build, the tally sequencing, the
 * signal bookkeeping and finalize().
 *
 * `isSignalled` is a GETTER, not a snapshot: run.js's signal handler mutates
 * its `signalled` local between awaits and the v4.0 code re-read it after every
 * chair launch. Passing the value instead would silently drop those aborts.
 */

const stage2 = require('./briefings-stage2');
const { parseChairVerdict } = require('./parse-stage2');
const runState = require('./run-state');
const { isAbortExit } = require('./run-stages');

/**
 * Chair fallback promotion (spec §4): the highest peers-only street-cred
 * model from `council stats` that is not a bench seat and not the failed
 * chair. "Highest street-cred" = BEST = numerically LOWEST mean rank
 * (deriveReliability's avgStreetCredPeersOnly; lower is better).
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function pickFallbackChair(statsRows, bench, failedChair) {
  const benchSet = new Set(bench);
  const candidates = (statsRows || [])
    .filter(r => !benchSet.has(r.model) && r.model !== failedChair
      && typeof r.avgStreetCredPeersOnly === 'number')
    .sort((a, b) => a.avgStreetCredPeersOnly - b.avgStreetCredPeersOnly);
  return candidates.length ? candidates[0].model : null;
}

/**
 * Chair chain (attempt → retry → ledger-promoted fallback → give up) plus the
 * single VERDICT-line repair re-prompt.
 * @param {object} ctx run.js's {o, launchers, addWave, overBudget, scratchDir}
 * @param {{packet: string, degraded: {value: boolean}, statsFn: Function,
 *   isSignalled: function(): (number|null)}} args
 * @returns {Promise<{aborted: number|null, chairLeg: object|null,
 *   actualChair: string|null, chairText: string|null,
 *   chairConformance: string, overallVerdict: string|null}>}
 */
async function runChair(ctx, { packet, degraded, statsFn, isSignalled }) {
  const { o, launchers, addWave, overBudget } = ctx;
  const now = () => new Date().toISOString();
  const bail = (code) => ({
    aborted: code, chairLeg: null, actualChair: null, chairText: null,
    chairConformance: 'clean', overallVerdict: null,
  });

  const attemptChair = async (model, waveId) => {
    runState.appendStageWave(o.runDir, 'chair', waveId);
    const solo = await launchers.launchSolo({
      model, prompt: packet, project: o.runDir, waveId,
      timeout: o.timeout, gateway: o.gateway, noValidateModel: o.noValidateModel,
    });
    addWave(solo.wave);
    const ok = solo.leg && solo.leg.status === 'complete'
      && solo.leg.summary && solo.leg.summary.trim();
    return { leg: ok ? solo.leg : null, exitCode: solo.exitCode };
  };

  let chairLeg = null;
  let actualChair = null;
  if (overBudget()) {
    // Ceiling hit after the tally is computable: skip the chair, write the
    // verdict with overallVerdict null, exit 2 (spec §4 degradation table).
    // Never abort in-flight legs for cost — this only stops NEW launches.
    degraded.value = true;
    runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'chair', { status: 'skipped', completedAt: now() });
  } else {
    runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'chair', { status: 'running', startedAt: now(), project: o.runDir });
    // Fallback chain (spec §4): retry same chair once → promote best
    // non-bench model from the ledger → give up (no Claude fallback headless).
    let attempt = await attemptChair(o.chair, `${o.runId}-ch1`);
    if (isAbortExit(attempt.exitCode) || isSignalled()) { return bail(attempt.exitCode || isSignalled()); }
    if (!attempt.leg && !overBudget()) {
      attempt = await attemptChair(o.chair, `${o.runId}-ch2`);
      if (isAbortExit(attempt.exitCode) || isSignalled()) { return bail(attempt.exitCode || isSignalled()); }
    }
    if (attempt.leg) { actualChair = o.chair; }
    else if (!overBudget()) {
      let statsRows = [];
      try { statsRows = statsFn(); } catch { /* no ledger yet */ }
      const fallback = pickFallbackChair(statsRows, o.models, o.chair);
      if (fallback) {
        attempt = await attemptChair(fallback, `${o.runId}-ch3`);
        if (isAbortExit(attempt.exitCode) || isSignalled()) { return bail(attempt.exitCode || isSignalled()); }
        if (attempt.leg) { actualChair = fallback; }
      }
    }
    chairLeg = attempt.leg;
    runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'chair',
      { status: chairLeg ? 'complete' : 'error', completedAt: now() });
    // The chair chain may have promoted a fallback (or given up) — checkpoint
    // the ACTUAL chair into run.json now so status/`--json`/the human summary
    // never report the originally-requested chair after a promotion. Mirrors
    // mkInput's actualChair || o.chair (a give-up with no actual chair keeps
    // the requested chair).
    runState.checkpoint(o.runDir, { chair: actualChair || o.chair });
  }
  const chairText = chairLeg ? chairLeg.summary : null;
  let chairConformance = 'clean';

  // ---- Chair VERDICT line (one repair re-prompt, spec §5) ----
  let overallVerdict = chairText ? parseChairVerdict(chairText) : null;
  if (chairText && !overallVerdict && !overBudget()) {
    runState.appendStageWave(o.runDir, 'chair', `${o.runId}-ch4`);
    const repair = await launchers.launchSolo({
      model: actualChair, prompt: stage2.buildChairRepairPrompt(),
      project: o.runDir, waveId: `${o.runId}-ch4`,
      timeout: o.timeout, gateway: o.gateway, noValidateModel: o.noValidateModel,
    });
    addWave(repair.wave);
    if (isAbortExit(repair.exitCode) || isSignalled()) { return bail(repair.exitCode || isSignalled()); }
    overallVerdict = parseChairVerdict((repair.leg && repair.leg.summary) || '');
    chairConformance = overallVerdict ? 'repaired' : 'unstructured';
  }
  // A completed chair whose verdict never parsed is 'unstructured' even when
  // the repair was skipped (e.g. the chair leg itself tripped --max-cost).
  if (chairText && !overallVerdict) { chairConformance = 'unstructured'; }
  if (!chairLeg || !overallVerdict) { degraded.value = true; } // spec table: exit 2 rows

  return {
    aborted: null, chairLeg, actualChair, chairText, chairConformance, overallVerdict,
  };
}

module.exports = { runChair, pickFallbackChair };
```

    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(o.runDir, 'chair-packet.md'), packet, { mode: 0o600 });

    const chairRes = await runChair(ctx, {
      packet, degraded, statsFn, isSignalled: () => signalled,
    });
    if (chairRes.aborted !== null) { return finalize(chairRes.aborted); }
    const { chairLeg, actualChair, chairText, chairConformance, overallVerdict } = chairRes;

    // ---- Final tally (chair row included) + ledger + artifacts ----
```

**(d)** `module.exports` is **UNCHANGED** — `module.exports = { runCouncil, pickFallbackChair, SIGNAL_EXIT };` still exports the re-imported `pickFallbackChair`, so `tests/council/run-chair.test.js` and every other consumer keep resolving it from `./run`.

Nothing else in `run.js` moves: `mkInput`, the provisional `tally()`, `buildChairPacket`, the final tally, the ledger append and `writeVerdictFiles` all stay put, so Task 4's edit anchors remain valid.

A parseable block ⇒ `ok:true`; each expected id present-and-valid gets its `{action, argument?/claim?}`; present-but-invalid (bad action enum, `defend` without `argument`, `amend` without `claim`) or absent ⇒ `{action:'no-response'}`. - [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — append to `src/council/parse-stage2.js` before `module.exports`, then extend the exports object:

```js
const DEBATE_ACTIONS = ['defend', 'amend', 'withdraw'];
const REVOTE_VERDICTS = ['agree', 'dispute', 'neutral'];

/**
 * Parse a debate defense solo (spec §5.4). Every expected id ends up in byId:
 * a present-and-valid entry keeps its action/argument/claim; anything absent or
 * individually invalid becomes {action:'no-response'} (the spec-mandated
 * "original stands" fallback). A block-level failure sets ok:false so the
 * caller issues exactly ONE repair; after that, every id is 'no-response'.
 * @param {string} text raw defense output (prose + trailing ```json block)
 * @param {string[]} expectedIds run-global ids sent to this raiser
 * @returns {{byId: Object, ok: boolean, errors: Array<{code, detail}>}}
 */
function parseDebateDefense(text, expectedIds) {
  const allNoResponse = () => {
    const byId = {};
    for (const id of expectedIds) { byId[id] = { action: 'no-response' }; }
    return byId;
  };
  const body = lastJsonBlock(text || '');
  if (body === null) {
    return { byId: allNoResponse(), ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'NO_FENCED_BLOCK', detail: 'no ```json block found' }] };
  }
  let parsed;
  try { parsed = JSON.parse(body); }
  catch (e) { return { byId: allNoResponse(), ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'NOT_PARSEABLE', detail: e.message }] }; }
  if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.responses)) {
    return { byId: allNoResponse(), ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'BAD_RESPONSES', detail: 'responses must be an array' }] };
  }
  const expected = new Set(expectedIds);
  const byId = allNoResponse();
  for (const r of parsed.responses) {
    const id = r && r.id;
    if (!expected.has(id)) { continue; }                 // unknown id ignored
    if (r.action === 'defend' && typeof r.argument === 'string' && r.argument.trim()) {
      byId[id] = { action: 'defend', argument: r.argument };
    } else if (r.action === 'amend' && typeof r.claim === 'string' && r.claim.trim()) {
      const entry = { action: 'amend', claim: r.claim };
      if (typeof r.argument === 'string' && r.argument.trim()) { entry.argument = r.argument; }
      byId[id] = entry;
    } else if (r.action === 'withdraw') {
      byId[id] = { action: 'withdraw' };
    } // else leave as no-response
  }
  return { byId, ok: true, errors: [] };
}

/**
 * Parse a re-vote leg (spec §5.4). byId holds ONLY present-and-valid ids; an
 * absent or invalid entry is omitted so the judge's original verdict stands.
 * Block-level failure sets ok:false (caller issues one repair, then originals
 * stand for all bundled ids).
 * @param {string} text raw re-vote output
 * @param {string[]} expectedIds bundled run-global ids
 * @returns {{byId: Object, ok: boolean, errors: Array<{code, detail}>}}
 */
function parseRevote(text, expectedIds) {
  const body = lastJsonBlock(text || '');
  if (body === null) {
    return { byId: {}, ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'NO_FENCED_BLOCK', detail: 'no ```json block found' }] };
  }
  let parsed;
  try { parsed = JSON.parse(body); }
  catch (e) { return { byId: {}, ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'NOT_PARSEABLE', detail: e.message }] }; }
  if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.revotes)) {
    return { byId: {}, ok: false, errors: [{ code: 'BAD_REVOTES', detail: 'revotes must be an array' }] };
  }
  const expected = new Set(expectedIds);
  const byId = {};
  for (const r of parsed.revotes) {
    const id = r && r.id;
    if (!expected.has(id) || !REVOTE_VERDICTS.includes(r.verdict)) { continue; }
    const entry = { verdict: r.verdict };
    if (typeof r.reason === 'string' && r.reason.trim()) { entry.reason = r.reason; }
    byId[id] = entry;
  }
  return { byId, ok: true, errors: [] };
}
```

Extend the exports (keep the existing keys):

```js
module.exports = {
  parseJudgeOutput, parseChairVerdict, CHAIR_VERDICTS, JUDGE_VERDICTS,
  parseDebateDefense, parseRevote, DEBATE_ACTIONS, REVOTE_VERDICTS,
};
```

### Task 2: `briefings-debate.js` templates + date-stamp on Stage-2/chair briefings

**Files:**
- Create: `src/council/briefings-debate.js`
- Modify: `src/council/briefings-stage2.js` (V4.0-NEW — add optional `date` to `buildJudgeBundle`/`buildChairPacket`; export a shared `dateLine`)
- Modify: `src/council/run-stages.js` (V4.0-NEW — pass `o.date` into the `buildJudgeBundle` call at line 149; it is the ONLY `buildJudgeBundle` call site in the codebase)
- Test: `tests/council/briefings-debate.test.js` (Create)
- Test: `tests/council/run-stages.test.js` (Modify — pin the date line in the written `bundle-stage2.md`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing (pure templates).
- Produces (consumed by `run-debate.js`, Task 4):
  - `buildDefenseBrief({findings, date}) → string` — `findings` = `[{id, claim, severity, location?, peerVerdicts: string[], disputeReasons: string[]}]`, both anonymized (no judge identities). `peerVerdicts` = the finding's REAL peer adjudication verdicts (`agree|dispute|neutral`) from the provisional tally record with the judge names stripped; the template renders the counts. `disputeReasons` = the peers' actual written reasons — Stage-2's adjudication JSON has no `reason` field today, so this is usually empty, and the template then says so instead of emitting a filler reason. **Never synthesize a stand-in reason string.** Opens with the no-tools preamble, then the date line, then the DEFEND/AMEND/WITHDRAW instruction and the strict `{"responses":[…]}` trailing-JSON contract (spec §5.3a).
  - `buildRevoteBundle({findings, date}) → string` — `findings` = `[{id, claim, severity, amended?: boolean, argument: string}]`. Opens with the no-tools preamble + date, then the re-adjudicate instruction and the `{"revotes":[…]}` contract (spec §5.3b). AMENDED findings mark the replacement claim.
  - `buildDefenseRepairPrompt({errors})`, `buildRevoteRepairPrompt({errors})` — one-shot re-prompts (spec §5.4 "1 repair").
  - `buildDebateAddendum({outcomes}) → string` — the chair-packet "Debate round outcomes" section (spec §5.3c): per finding, original claim → defense action (defended / amended with both texts / withdrawn / no response) → per-judge re-vote changes (before → after). Each outcome carries `priorVerdicts: {judge: 'agree'|'dispute'|'neutral'}` — the finding's REAL Stage-2 adjudications from the provisional tally record — and `revotes: {judge: verdict}`; the template derives `before` from `priorVerdicts` so no caller can assume one. **A re-vote recipient qualified by disputing SOME bundled finding, so its prior verdict on THIS finding is frequently `agree` or `neutral` — hardcoding `before: 'dispute'` misreports the chair packet.** `outcomes` is built from `debate.json` + both tally records.
  - `DEBATE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE` (verbatim, first line of both briefs).
- Also: `buildJudgeBundle({reviews, findings, date?})` and `buildChairPacket({reviews, rankings, adjudications, tierCounts, date?})` now prepend `Today's date is <date>.` when `date` is given (spec §4.3). `dateLine(date)` is exported from `briefings-stage2.js` and reused by `briefings-debate.js`. The optional param on its own is inert: `src/council/run-stages.js:149` is the only `buildJudgeBundle` call site anywhere, so **this** task also passes `o.date` there (Step 3) — otherwise the Stage-2 judge bundle is never date-stamped and spec §4.3 fails silently. The `buildChairPacket` call site stays in `run.js` (Task 0.5 moves only the chair chain) and is passed `o.date` in Task 4.

    expect(brief).toContain('"action": "amend"');

  });
  test('lists the finding, the REAL peer split and the anonymized reasons, no judge identities', () => {
    expect(brief).toContain('A1');

    expect(brief).toContain('the backoff caps at 5 attempts');
    expect(brief).toContain('Peer verdicts (anonymized): 2 dispute, 1 agree, 0 neutral.');

});

describe('repair prompts', () => {

    expect(t.startsWith('Do NOT use any tools')).toBe(true);

      priorVerdicts: { gpt: 'dispute', qwen: 'neutral' }, revotes: { gpt: 'agree', qwen: 'agree' } },
    { id: 'B2', originalClaim: 'b', action: 'withdrawn', amendedClaim: null,
      priorVerdicts: {}, revotes: {} },

    });

});

function buildJudgeBundle({ reviews, findings, date }) {
  const findingLines = findings.map(f => `${f.id} [${f.severity}] ${f.claim}`).join('\n');
  const reviewBlocks = reviews.map(r => `--- ${r.label} ---\n${r.text}`).join('\n\n');
  const parts = [JUDGE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE];
  if (date) { parts.push(dateLine(date)); }
  parts.push(
    'You are judging the anonymized peer reviews below. Do two things:',
    'Task A — Rank: order the reviews from most to least accurate and insightful.',
    'Task B — Adjudicate: for EVERY finding id listed below, state agree, dispute, or ' +
    'neutral with your reasoning in prose.',
    JUDGE_OUTPUT_CONTRACT,
    '--- FINDINGS INDEX (run-global ids) ---',
    findingLines,
    reviewBlocks,
  );
  return parts.join('\n\n');
}

In `buildChairPacket`, add `date` and inject the date line after the preamble:

```js
function buildChairPacket({ reviews, rankings, adjudications, tierCounts, date }) {
  const reviewBlocks = reviews.map(r => `--- Review by ${r.model} ---\n${r.text}`).join('\n\n');
  const rankingLines = rankings.map(r => `${r.judge}: ${JSON.stringify(r.order)}`).join('\n');
  const adjLines = adjudications.map(a => `${a.findingId} — ${a.judge}: ${a.verdict}`).join('\n');
  const tiers = JSON.stringify(tierCounts);
  const parts = [CHAIR_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE];
  if (date) { parts.push(dateLine(date)); }
  parts.push(
    'You are the council chair. Write the synthesized verdict across the reviews, ' +
    'rankings, and adjudications below. Weigh each reviewer\'s findings by their ' +
    'peer-validated standing (rank position and adjudication pattern), distinguish ' +
    'findings the bench broadly endorsed from contested or singleton claims, and ' +
    'arrive at an overall assessment of the material.',
    `Deterministic tier counts (peers-only cascade): ${tiers}`,
    '--- STAGE-1 REVIEWS (de-anonymized) ---',
    reviewBlocks,
    '--- PEER RANKINGS (judge: order, best first) ---',
    rankingLines,
    '--- PER-FINDING ADJUDICATIONS ---',
    adjLines,
    VERDICT_SCALE_ADDENDUM,
  );
  return parts.join('\n\n');
}
```

Then create `src/council/briefings-debate.js`:

```js
// src/council/briefings-debate.js
'use strict';

/**
 * @module council/briefings-debate
 * Pure Stage-2.5 (debate) template builders for the headless council engine
 * (spec §5.3). Adapted from skills/second-opinion/SEAT-BRIEFS.md § Rebuttal-round
 * templates, hardened from the line-based contract to the v4.0 Stage-2
 * trailing-JSON style (prose first, one fenced ```json block last, nothing
 * after it). Every brief opens with the no-tools preamble and the date stamp
 * (spec §4.3). No IO, no model calls.
 */

const { dateLine } = require('./briefings-stage2');

const DEBATE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE =
  'Do NOT use any tools or read any files; everything is in this message; ' +
  'begin immediately with the JSON block.';

/** Defense trailing-JSON contract (spec §5.3a). */
const DEFENSE_CONTRACT = [
  'End your response with a fenced ```json block, with no text after it:',
  '',
  '```json',
  '{',
  '  "responses": [',
  '    { "id": "A1", "action": "defend", "argument": "<strongest evidence-based defense, one paragraph max>" },',
  '    { "id": "A3", "action": "amend", "claim": "<full corrected replacement claim>", "argument": "<why, one sentence>" },',
  '    { "id": "B2", "action": "withdraw" }',
  '  ]',
  '}',
  '```',
  '',
  'Every listed finding id must appear exactly once. `defend` requires `argument`; ' +
  '`amend` requires `claim`; `withdraw` requires neither.',
].join('\n');

/** Re-vote trailing-JSON contract (spec §5.3b). */
const REVOTE_CONTRACT = [
  'End your response with a fenced ```json block, with no text after it:',
  '',
  '```json',
  '{',
  '  "revotes": [',
  '    { "id": "A1", "verdict": "agree", "reason": "<one line>" },',
  '    { "id": "A3", "verdict": "dispute", "reason": "<one line>" }',
  '  ]',
  '}',
  '```',
  '',
  '`verdict` is one of agree | dispute | neutral. Every listed finding id must ' +
  'appear exactly once.',
].join('\n');

/** Count the anonymized peer verdicts carried on a finding. */
function verdictCounts(list) {
  const c = { dispute: 0, agree: 0, neutral: 0 };
  for (const v of list || []) { if (c[v] !== undefined) { c[v] += 1; } }
  return c;
}

function findingBlockDefense(f) {
  const c = verdictCounts(f.peerVerdicts);
  const reasons = (f.disputeReasons || []).filter(Boolean);
  const loc = f.location ? ` @ ${f.location}` : '';
  // Stage-2's adjudication JSON has no `reason` field, so written reasons are usually
  // absent. Report the REAL anonymized peer split instead of inventing a reason string.
  const why = reasons.length
    ? `  Peers disputed it for:\n${reasons.map(r => `    - ${r}`).join('\n')}`
    : '  No written dispute reasons were captured.';
  return `- ${f.id} [${f.severity}]${loc}: ${f.claim}\n`
    + `  Peer verdicts (anonymized): ${c.dispute} dispute, ${c.agree} agree, ${c.neutral} neutral.\n`
    + why;
}

function findingBlockRevote(f) {
  const mark = f.amended ? ' **AMENDED**' : '';
  return `- ${f.id} [${f.severity}]${mark}: ${f.claim}\n  Raiser's response: ${f.argument}`;
}

/** One defense solo per raiser (spec §5.3a). */
function buildDefenseBrief({ findings, date }) {
  const parts = [DEBATE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE];
  if (date) { parts.push(dateLine(date)); }
  parts.push(
    'You reviewed an artifact and raised the findings below. Peer reviewers ' +
    '(anonymous) disputed them for the stated reasons. For EACH finding decide: ' +
    'DEFEND it with evidence, AMEND it with corrected replacement text, or WITHDRAW ' +
    'it. Withdraw anything you cannot defend with evidence — an unsupported repeat ' +
    'of the original claim is weaker than a withdrawal.',
    findings.map(findingBlockDefense).join('\n\n'),
    DEFENSE_CONTRACT,
  );
  return parts.join('\n\n');
}

/** One shared re-vote bundle, fanned out to disputing judges (spec §5.3b). */
function buildRevoteBundle({ findings, date }) {
  const parts = [DEBATE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE];
  if (date) { parts.push(dateLine(date)); }
  parts.push(
    'You previously adjudicated findings on this artifact and disputed at least ' +
    'one of those below. The (anonymous) raiser has now responded. Re-adjudicate ' +
    'ONLY the findings listed, in light of each response. Changing your verdict ' +
    'when the defense is convincing is good judging, not weakness; so is holding ' +
    'your dispute when it isn\'t.',
    findings.map(findingBlockRevote).join('\n\n'),
    REVOTE_CONTRACT,
  );
  return parts.join('\n\n');
}

function repair(kind, contract, errors) {
  const lines = (errors || []).map(e => `- ${e.code}: ${e.detail}`).join('\n');
  return [
    'Do NOT use any tools or read any files; everything is in this message; begin ' +
    'immediately with the JSON block.',
    `Your previous ${kind} response's trailing JSON failed validation with these errors:`,
    lines,
    'Re-emit ONLY the corrected JSON block as a single fenced ```json block:',
    contract,
  ].join('\n\n');
}

function buildDefenseRepairPrompt({ errors }) { return repair('defense', DEFENSE_CONTRACT, errors); }
function buildRevoteRepairPrompt({ errors }) { return repair('re-vote', REVOTE_CONTRACT, errors); }

/**
 * Chair-packet "Debate round outcomes" section (spec §5.3c). De-anonymized —
 * built by run.js from debate.json + both tally records. `priorVerdicts` is the
 * finding's ACTUAL Stage-2 adjudication map from the provisional tally record and
 * `revotes` the map of judges who re-voted; `before` is derived here so no caller
 * can assume it. A re-vote recipient qualified by disputing SOME bundled finding,
 * so its prior verdict on THIS finding is often 'agree' or 'neutral'.
 * @param {{outcomes: Array<{id, originalClaim, action, amendedClaim,
 *   priorVerdicts: Object<string,string>, revotes: Object<string,string>}>}} args
 */
function buildDebateAddendum({ outcomes }) {
  const blocks = outcomes.map(o => {
    const head = `- ${o.id}: "${o.originalClaim}" → ${o.action}`;
    const amended = o.action === 'amended' && o.amendedClaim ? `\n  Amended claim: "${o.amendedClaim}"` : '';
    const prior = o.priorVerdicts || {};
    const revotes = o.revotes || {};
    const judges = Object.keys(revotes);
    const changes = judges.length
      ? '\n  Re-vote changes: ' + judges.map(
        j => `${j}: ${prior[j] || 'no prior verdict'} → ${revotes[j]}`).join('; ')
      : '\n  Re-vote changes: none';
    return head + amended + changes;
  }).join('\n');
  return ['--- Debate round outcomes ---', blocks].join('\n\n');
}

module.exports = {
  DEBATE_NO_TOOLS_PREAMBLE, DEFENSE_CONTRACT, REVOTE_CONTRACT,
  buildDefenseBrief, buildRevoteBundle,
  buildDefenseRepairPrompt, buildRevoteRepairPrompt, buildDebateAddendum,
};
```

### Task 3: `debate.js` — pure final-tally reassembly

**Files:**
- Create: `src/council/debate.js`
- Modify: `src/council/ledger.js` (one-line guard: debate legs are skipped by the runStats→ledger join, so they can never overwrite a bench model's ledger row)
- Test: `tests/council/debate.test.js` (Create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing (pure; DI-free — spec §5.5).
- Produces (consumed by `run-debate.js` / `run.js`, Task 4):
  - `applyDebate({ provisionalRecord, tallyInput, defenseByRaiser, revoteByJudge }) → { input, debateFindings }` — returns a NEW tally-input object with:
    - **Amend:** the finding's `claim` replaced in `input.findings` (`id`/`raiser`/`severity` unchanged).
    - **Withdraw:** the finding STAYS in `input.findings` (unchanged text).
    - **Re-vote replacement:** for each re-vote-wave judge J and bundled finding F with a valid re-vote, J's `adjudications` entry `{findingId:F, judge:J, verdict}` is REPLACED (agree/dispute/neutral). A judge re-votes every bundled finding it was sent, including ones it had not disputed (stateless legs; spec §5.5 rejected-alternative note). Raiser self-votes pass through untouched (tally excludes them).
    - `debateFindings` = the `debate.json` `findings[]` rows: `{id, raiser, action, argument?, claim?, previousTier}` (present-tense action from the parser).
  - `decorateRecord(record, debateFindings) → record` — injects the additive past-tense `debate: {action, previousTier}` onto each matching `record.findings[]` entry (maps `defend→defended`, `amend→amended`, `withdraw→withdrawn`, `no-response→no-response`); returns the same record (mutated in place is fine — the caller owns it). Findings not in the debate get no `debate` key.
  - `debateRunStatsRows({ defenseLegs, revoteLegs }) → runStats[]` — `{model, role:'rebuttal'|'revote', wasChair:false, conformance, status, durationMs, usage}` rows appended to the final tally input's `runStats` (spec §5.5). `appendRun` keys the ROW SET off `meta.models` (bench), so the ledger still gets exactly one row per (run × model) — but `buildLedgerRows` joins `runStats` onto those rows through a last-wins `new Map(runStats.map(r => [r.model, r]))` (`ledger.js:20`), and a debate leg is an extra leg by a model that ALREADY has a bench row. Unguarded, that row silently overwrites the bench row's `role`/`wasChair`/`conformance` in the ledger. Task 3 therefore also adds the one-line guard in `ledger.js` that drops `DEBATE_ROLES` rows from that lookup: the debate legs stay in `runStats` (cost rollups, report cost rows) and stay out of the ledger — spec §5.5's stated intent.
  - `DEBATE_ROLES = new Set(['rebuttal', 'revote'])` — the role names the ledger join skips (single source of truth, consumed by `ledger.js`).
  - `PAST_TENSE = { defend:'defended', amend:'amended', withdraw:'withdrawn', 'no-response':'no-response' }`.

'use strict';
const { applyDebate, decorateRecord, debateRunStatsRows, PAST_TENSE, DEBATE_ROLES } =
  require('../../src/council/debate');

      { id: 'A2', raiser: 'gemini', severity: 'minor', claim: 'log leak' },
      { id: 'B1', raiser: 'gpt', severity: 'nit', claim: 'typo' },

    adjudications: [

      { findingId: 'A1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'A2', judge: 'gpt', verdict: 'dispute' },

    rankings: [{ judge: 'gpt', order: ['gemini', 'qwen'] }, { judge: 'qwen', order: ['gemini', 'gpt'] }],

  };
}

describe('applyDebate — amend replaces claim only', () => {

describe('applyDebate — re-vote replaces only wave judges\' entries on bundled ids', () => {
  test('gpt flips A1 dispute→agree; qwen unchanged; raiser self-vote untouched', () => {
    const { input } = applyDebate({
      provisionalRecord: null, tallyInput: baseInput(),

    const gptA1 = input.adjudications.find(a => a.findingId === 'A1' && a.judge === 'gpt');
    const qwenA1 = input.adjudications.find(a => a.findingId === 'A1' && a.judge === 'qwen');
    expect(gptA1.verdict).toBe('agree');

  test('a re-vote on an id the judge never disputed is still applied (stateless legs)', () => {

  });
});

describe('decorateRecord — additive past-tense debate field', () => {
  test('maps present-tense action to past-tense and attaches previousTier', () => {
    const record = { findings: [
      { id: 'A1', tier: 'Confirmed' }, { id: 'A2', tier: 'Singleton' }, { id: 'B1', tier: 'Singleton' },
    ] };
    const debateFindings = [
      { id: 'A1', action: 'defend', previousTier: 'Contested' },
      { id: 'A2', action: 'withdraw', previousTier: 'Disputed' },
    ];
    decorateRecord(record, debateFindings);
    expect(record.findings[0].debate).toEqual({ action: 'defended', previousTier: 'Contested' });
    expect(record.findings[1].debate).toEqual({ action: 'withdrawn', previousTier: 'Disputed' });
    expect(record.findings[2].debate).toBeUndefined(); // not in the debate
  });
  test('PAST_TENSE maps every action', () => {
    expect(PAST_TENSE).toEqual({ defend: 'defended', amend: 'amended', withdraw: 'withdrawn', 'no-response': 'no-response' });
  });
});

describe('debateRunStatsRows', () => {
  test('emits rebuttal + revote rows tagged with the debate roles', () => {
    const rows = debateRunStatsRows({
      defenseLegs: [{ model: 'gemini', status: 'complete', durationMs: 50, usage: null, conformance: 'clean' }],
      revoteLegs: [{ model: 'gpt', status: 'complete', durationMs: 60, usage: null, conformance: 'unstructured' }],
    });
    expect(rows).toEqual([
      { model: 'gemini', role: 'rebuttal', wasChair: false, conformance: 'clean', status: 'complete', durationMs: 50, usage: null },
      { model: 'gpt', role: 'revote', wasChair: false, conformance: 'unstructured', status: 'complete', durationMs: 60, usage: null },
    ]);
    expect([...DEBATE_ROLES].sort()).toEqual(['rebuttal', 'revote']);
  });

  // A debate leg is an EXTRA leg by a model that already has a bench row. buildLedgerRows
  // joins runStats by model with a last-wins Map, so without the ledger.js guard these rows
  // would clobber the bench row's role/wasChair/conformance. Pin that they do not.
  test('debate legs never overwrite a bench model ledger row', () => {
    const input = baseInput();
    input.runStats = [
      { model: 'gemini', role: 'seat', wasChair: false, conformance: 'clean', status: 'complete', durationMs: 100, usage: null },
      { model: 'gpt', role: 'critic', wasChair: false, conformance: 'clean', status: 'complete', durationMs: 110, usage: null },
      ...debateRunStatsRows({
        defenseLegs: [{ model: 'gemini', status: 'complete', durationMs: 50, usage: null, conformance: 'unstructured' }],
        revoteLegs: [{ model: 'gpt', status: 'complete', durationMs: 60, usage: null, conformance: 'repaired' }],
      }),
    ];
    const rows = buildLedgerRows(tally(input));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(3);               // one per meta.models entry — debate legs add none
    const gemini = rows.find(r => r.model === 'gemini');
    const gpt = rows.find(r => r.model === 'gpt');
    expect(gemini.role).toBe('seat');           // NOT 'rebuttal'
    expect(gemini.conformance).toBe('clean');   // NOT the defense leg's 'unstructured'
    expect(gpt.role).toBe('critic');            // NOT 'revote'
    expect(gpt.conformance).toBe('clean');      // NOT the re-vote leg's 'repaired'
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

Create `src/council/debate.js`:

```js
// src/council/debate.js
'use strict';

/**
 * @module council/debate
 * PURE final-tally reassembly for headless debate mode (spec §5.5). DI-free —
 * takes the provisional tally input + parsed defense/re-vote maps and returns a
 * new tally input plus the debate.json findings rows. run.js then re-runs
 * tally() on the returned input and decorates the record. Keeps run.js under the
 * line gate.
 */

const PAST_TENSE = { defend: 'defended', amend: 'amended', withdraw: 'withdrawn', 'no-response': 'no-response' };

// runStats roles the ledger join must skip (ledger.js): a debate leg is an extra leg by an
// already-benched model, never an extra ledger row and never that model's ledger identity.
const DEBATE_ROLES = new Set(['rebuttal', 'revote']);

/**
 * Reassemble the tally input after the debate round.
 * @param {{tallyInput: object, provisionalRecord: object|null,
 *   defenseByRaiser: Object<string, Object<string, object>>,
 *   revoteByJudge: Object<string, Object<string, {verdict, reason?}>>}} args
 * @returns {{input: object, debateFindings: Array}}
 */
function applyDebate({ tallyInput, defenseByRaiser, revoteByJudge }) {
  // Deep-ish clone the mutable arrays we touch (findings + adjudications).
  const findings = tallyInput.findings.map(f => ({ ...f }));
  const adjudications = tallyInput.adjudications.map(a => ({ ...a }));

  // Flatten the per-raiser defense map to a per-id lookup + debate.json rows.
  const byId = {};
  const debateFindings = [];
  for (const [raiser, perId] of Object.entries(defenseByRaiser || {})) {
    for (const [id, resp] of Object.entries(perId)) {
      byId[id] = resp;
      const src = findings.find(f => f.id === id) || {};
      const row = { id, raiser, action: resp.action, previousTier: src.previousTier || null };
      if (resp.argument) { row.argument = resp.argument; }
      if (resp.claim) { row.claim = resp.claim; }
      debateFindings.push(row);
    }
  }

  // Amend: swap claim text in place. Withdraw/defend/no-response: findings[] unchanged.
  for (const f of findings) {
    const resp = byId[f.id];
    if (resp && resp.action === 'amend' && typeof resp.claim === 'string') { f.claim = resp.claim; }
    delete f.previousTier; // provisional-only scratch field, never written to tally input
  }

  // Re-vote replacement: replace the wave judge's entry on each bundled id.
  for (const [judge, perId] of Object.entries(revoteByJudge || {})) {
    for (const [id, rv] of Object.entries(perId)) {
      const entry = adjudications.find(a => a.findingId === id && a.judge === judge);
      if (entry) { entry.verdict = rv.verdict; }
      else { adjudications.push({ findingId: id, judge, verdict: rv.verdict }); }
    }
  }

  return { input: { ...tallyInput, findings, adjudications }, debateFindings };
}

/**
 * Inject the additive past-tense debate decoration onto the tally record's
 * findings (spec §5.6). Mutates + returns the record.
 * @param {object} record tally() output
 * @param {Array<{id, action, previousTier}>} debateFindings
 * @returns {object} record
 */
function decorateRecord(record, debateFindings) {
  const byId = new Map((debateFindings || []).map(d => [d.id, d]));
  for (const f of record.findings) {
    const d = byId.get(f.id);
    if (d) { f.debate = { action: PAST_TENSE[d.action] || 'no-response', previousTier: d.previousTier }; }
  }
  return record;
}

/**
 * runStats rows for the debate legs (spec §5.5). role is 'rebuttal' | 'revote';
 * these legs never enter meta.models, so the ledger stays one row per (run×model),
 * and ledger.js skips DEBATE_ROLES when joining runStats so a debate leg cannot
 * overwrite the bench row's role/wasChair/conformance on that model's ledger row.
 * @param {{defenseLegs: Array, revoteLegs: Array}} args leg metadata
 * @returns {Array<object>}
 */
function debateRunStatsRows({ defenseLegs, revoteLegs }) {
  const mk = (role) => (l) => ({
    model: l.model, role, wasChair: false, conformance: l.conformance || 'clean',
    status: l.status || 'unknown',
    durationMs: typeof l.durationMs === 'number' ? l.durationMs : null,
    usage: l.usage || null,
  });
  return [...(defenseLegs || []).map(mk('rebuttal')), ...(revoteLegs || []).map(mk('revote'))];
}

module.exports = { applyDebate, decorateRecord, debateRunStatsRows, PAST_TENSE, DEBATE_ROLES };
```

Then add the ledger guard to `src/council/ledger.js`. Without it the new rows clobber the bench
rows, because `buildLedgerRows` builds its runStats lookup with a last-wins `Map`:

```js
// src/council/ledger.js — alongside the existing requires at the top of the file:
const { DEBATE_ROLES } = require('./debate');

// …and inside buildLedgerRows(), replace the runStats lookup (today ledger.js:20):
//   const rs = new Map(runStats.map(r => [r.model, r]));
  const rs = new Map(runStats.filter(r => !DEBATE_ROLES.has(r.role)).map(r => [r.model, r]));
```

The debate legs stay in `runStats` (cost rollups, report cost rows) and stay out of the ledger
join — spec §5.5's stated intent. No require cycle: `debate.js` requires nothing.

### Task 4: `run-debate.js` orchestration + wire `--debate` into `run.js`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/council/run-debate.js`
- Modify: `src/council/run.js` (V4.0-NEW — insert the debate branch between Stage-2 and the chair; re-verify the anchor lines from Task 0 Step 3)
- Modify: `src/council/run-launch.js` (V4.0-NEW — add `materializeDebate(runDir, legs, prefix)` for `rebuttal-<model>.md`/`revote-<model>.md`; already has `sanitizeName`)
- Modify: `tests/council/helpers/fake-launchers.js` (V4.0-NEW — add scripted defense/re-vote outputs)
- Test: `tests/council/run-debate.test.js` (Create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `applyDebate`/`decorateRecord`/`debateRunStatsRows` (Task 3); `buildDefenseBrief`/`buildRevoteBundle`/`buildDefenseRepairPrompt`/`buildRevoteRepairPrompt`/`buildDebateAddendum` (Task 2); `parseDebateDefense`/`parseRevote` (Task 1); the DI launchers `launchSolo`/`launchWave` (`run-launch.js`, C6); `tally` (C2); `run-state.updateStage` (C8).
- Produces:
  - `runDebate(ctx, { provisionalRecord, tallyInput }) → Promise<{ debatedInput, debateFindings, debateSummary, addendumOutcomes, defenseLegs, revoteLegs, verdictChanges, degraded, aborted }>` — the whole Stage-2.5 sequence. `ctx` is run.js's `{o, launchers, addWave, overBudget, scratchDir}`. Returns everything run.js needs to build the chair packet (with `buildDebateAddendum`), the final tally input, the `debate.json` artifact, and the `run.json` `debate` summary. **`degraded: boolean`** — true when a defense solo is dead/unstructured-after-repair, the re-vote wave is partial or fully dead, or a warranted re-vote was skipped by the cost ceiling (spec §5.7); run.js sets `degraded.value = true` (exit 2) when it is set. **`aborted: number|null`** — when a launch returns an abort exit code (130/143) mid-debate, `runDebate` short-circuits and returns `{ aborted: <code>, contested, disputed }` (all other fields absent); run.js checkpoints a schema-valid `debate` summary (`outcome:'ran'` plus those two counts) and then finalizes 'aborted' immediately, writing NO tally-final and NO ledger row. The summary write is mandatory under the **writer contract** (Shared contracts: whenever the `debate` key is present it always carries a valid `outcome`), so an aborted round must never leave `debate:{enabled:true}` half-written. Task 5's schema deliberately requires only `enabled` — a half-written object would still validate — so the writer must not lean on the schema to catch this.
  - `nothingToDebate(provisionalRecord) → boolean` — true when Contested+Disputed == 0 OR `judged === false`.
  - `disputingJudges(provisionalRecord, bundledIds) → string[]` — judges whose provisional adjudications contain ≥1 `dispute` on any bundled id.
  - `materializeDebate(runDir, legs, prefix)` (in run-launch.js) — writes `<prefix>-<sanitizeName(model)>.md` per leg with a non-empty summary; returns the written descriptors.
- run.js behavior when `o.debate` is true and there IS something to debate: `stage2 → tally-provisional (no ledger) → runDebate → chair packet (with addendum) → tally-final (ledger unless lenses) → verdict/report`; `run.json` gets the three debate stages + the top-level `debate` summary + `debate.json`. When `o.debate` is false OR nothing to debate: the v4.0 flow is used verbatim (nothing-to-debate records `debate.outcome:'nothing-to-debate'` and still ledgers the unchanged input).
