// tests/council/run-debate.test.js
'use strict';
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const fs = require('fs');
const { runDebate, nothingToDebate, disputingJudges } = require('../../src/council/run-debate');
const { tally } = require('../../src/council/tally');

function provisionalInput() {
  return {
    meta: { runId: 'r', models: ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], chair: 'deepseek', claudeInCouncil: false, date: '2026-07-19' },
    findings: [
      { id: 'A1', raiser: 'gemini', severity: 'major', claim: 'infinite retry' },  // disputed by gpt+qwen → Disputed
      { id: 'B1', raiser: 'gpt', severity: 'nit', claim: 'typo' },                  // agreed → Confirmed
    ],
    adjudications: [
      { findingId: 'A1', judge: 'gpt', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'A1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'B1', judge: 'gemini', verdict: 'agree' },
      { findingId: 'B1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'agree' },
    ],
    rankings: [{ judge: 'gpt', order: ['gemini', 'qwen'] }, { judge: 'qwen', order: ['gemini', 'gpt'] }],
    runStats: [],
  };
}

// Fake launchers: defense solo defends A1; re-vote wave has gpt flip to agree, qwen hold dispute.
// Mirrors the run-launch.js DI contract: launchSolo → {wave, exitCode, leg}; launchWave → {wave, exitCode}.
function fakeLaunchers(script, seen) {
  return {
    launchSolo: async (opts) => { if (seen) { seen.push(opts); } return { ...script.solos[opts.waveId], exitCode: 0 }; },
    launchWave: async (opts) => { if (seen) { seen.push(opts); } return { wave: script.waves[opts.waveId], exitCode: 0 }; },
  };
}
function leg(model, summary) { return { model, modelInput: model, status: 'complete', summary, taskId: `${model}-t` }; }
function wave(legs) { return { status: 'complete', legs }; }
const defenseOut = (resp) => `Defending.\n\`\`\`json\n${JSON.stringify({ responses: resp })}\n\`\`\`\n`;
const revoteOut = (rv) => `Re-voting.\n\`\`\`json\n${JSON.stringify({ revotes: rv })}\n\`\`\`\n`;

function ctxFor(tmp, launchers) {
  return {
    o: { runId: 'r', runDir: tmp, timeout: 10, gateway: 'auto', date: '2026-07-19', maxCost: null, noCostGate: false },
    launchers, addWave: () => {}, overBudget: () => false, scratchDir: path.join(tmp, '_scratch'),
  };
}

describe('nothingToDebate / disputingJudges', () => {
  test('nothingToDebate true when no Contested+Disputed', () => {
    const rec = tally({ ...provisionalInput(), findings: [{ id: 'B1', raiser: 'gpt', severity: 'nit', claim: 't' }],
      adjudications: [{ findingId: 'B1', judge: 'gemini', verdict: 'agree' }, { findingId: 'B1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'agree' }] });
    expect(nothingToDebate(rec)).toBe(true);
  });
  test('disputingJudges picks judges with a dispute on a bundled id', () => {
    const rec = tally(provisionalInput());
    expect(disputingJudges(rec, ['A1']).sort()).toEqual(['gpt', 'qwen']);
  });
});

describe('runDebate — happy path (defend + partial re-vote flip)', () => {
  let tmp, result, launched;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'run-debate-'));
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmp, '_scratch'), { recursive: true });
    const input = provisionalInput();
    const provisionalRecord = tally(input);
    launched = [];                       // every launch's opts, so the briefs can be asserted
    const launchers = fakeLaunchers({
      solos: { 'r-d1': { wave: wave([leg('gemini', defenseOut([{ id: 'A1', action: 'defend', argument: 'caps at 5' }]))]),
                         leg: leg('gemini', defenseOut([{ id: 'A1', action: 'defend', argument: 'caps at 5' }])) } },
      waves: { 'r-rv': wave([leg('gpt', revoteOut([{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
                             leg('qwen', revoteOut([{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'dispute' }]))]) },
    }, launched);
    result = await runDebate(ctxFor(tmp, launchers), { provisionalRecord, tallyInput: input });
  });

  test('debateSummary counts the round', () => {
    expect(result.debateSummary.outcome).toBe('ran');
    expect(result.debateSummary.disputed).toBe(1);
    expect(result.debateSummary.defended).toBe(1);
    expect(result.debateSummary.revoteJudges).toBe(2);
  });

  test('the debated tally flips A1 from Disputed toward Contested (gpt agreed)', () => {
    const rec = tally(result.debatedInput);
    const a1 = rec.findings.find(f => f.id === 'A1');
    // peers now a=1 (gpt agree), d=1 (qwen dispute) → Contested
    expect(a1.basis).toEqual({ a: 1, d: 1, n: 0 });
    expect(a1.tier).toBe('Contested');
    expect(result.verdictChanges).toBe(1);
  });

  test('writes rebuttal-, revote- and the shared re-vote bundle artifacts', () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'rebuttal-gemini.md'))).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'revote-gpt.md'))).toBe(true);
    // spec §5.1 names revote-bundle.md a run-dir artifact — audit parity with
    // briefing-stage1.md / bundle-stage2.md / chair-packet.md.
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'revote-bundle.md'))).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'debate.json'))).toBe(true);
  });

  test('the chair addendum carries the REAL prior verdicts and re-votes, not assumed ones', () => {
    const a1 = result.addendumOutcomes.find(o => o.id === 'A1');
    expect(a1.priorVerdicts).toEqual({ gpt: 'dispute', qwen: 'dispute' });
    expect(a1.revotes).toEqual({ gpt: 'agree', qwen: 'dispute' });
  });

  test('the defense brief carries the REAL peer split, not an empty one', () => {
    const d1 = launched.find(o => o.waveId === 'r-d1');
    expect(d1.prompt).toContain('Peer verdicts (anonymized): 2 dispute, 0 agree, 0 neutral.');
  });
});

describe('runDebate — defense solos run CONCURRENTLY (spec §5.1)', () => {
  test('two raisers are in flight at the same time', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'run-debate-conc-'));
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmp, '_scratch'), { recursive: true });
    const input = provisionalInput();
    // Make B1 Disputed too, so gpt is a SECOND raiser with its own defense solo.
    input.adjudications = [
      { findingId: 'A1', judge: 'gpt', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'A1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'B1', judge: 'gemini', verdict: 'dispute' },
      { findingId: 'B1', judge: 'qwen', verdict: 'dispute' },
    ];
    const provisionalRecord = tally(input);
    let inFlight = 0;
    let maxInFlight = 0;
    const launchers = {
      launchSolo: async ({ waveId, model }) => {
        inFlight += 1;
        maxInFlight = Math.max(maxInFlight, inFlight);
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 5));
        inFlight -= 1;
        // Both withdraw ⇒ nothing defended/amended ⇒ the re-vote wave never launches.
        const out = defenseOut([{ id: waveId === 'r-d1' ? 'A1' : 'B1', action: 'withdraw' }]);
        return { wave: wave([leg(model, out)]), leg: leg(model, out), exitCode: 0 };
      },
      launchWave: async () => { throw new Error('no re-vote wave expected'); },
    };
    await runDebate(ctxFor(tmp, launchers), { provisionalRecord, tallyInput: input });
    expect(maxInFlight).toBe(2);   // a sequential `await` in a for-loop caps this at 1
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

Create `src/council/run-debate.js`:

```js
// src/council/run-debate.js
'use strict';

/**
 * @module council/run-debate
 * Impure Stage-2.5 orchestration for headless debate mode (spec §5.1). Launches
 * the defense mini-wave (one solo per raiser) and the re-vote mini-wave (one
 * fanout to disputing judges), parses each with one bounded repair, then hands
 * off to the pure reassembly in ./debate.js. Kept out of run.js for the 300-line
 * gate. Launchers are injected via ctx (repo DI pattern).
 */

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const dbrief = require('./briefings-debate');
const { parseDebateDefense, parseRevote } = require('./parse-stage2');
const { applyDebate, debateRunStatsRows } = require('./debate');
const { materializeDebate } = require('./run-launch');
const { tally } = require('./tally');
const { isAbortExit } = require('./run-stages');
const runState = require('./run-state');

/** True when there is nothing to challenge (spec §5.1). */
function nothingToDebate(provisionalRecord) {
  if (!provisionalRecord || provisionalRecord.judged === false) { return true; }
  const n = provisionalRecord.findings.filter(f => f.tier === 'Contested' || f.tier === 'Disputed').length;
  return n === 0;
}

/** Judges whose provisional adjudications dispute at least one bundled id. */
function disputingJudges(provisionalRecord, bundledIds) {
  const ids = new Set(bundledIds);
  const judges = new Set();
  for (const f of provisionalRecord.findings) {
    if (!ids.has(f.id)) { continue; }
    for (const adj of f.adjudications || []) {
      if (adj.verdict === 'dispute') { judges.add(adj.judge); }
    }
  }
  return [...judges];
}

/** Group Contested+Disputed findings by raiser (defense targets). */
function debateTargets(provisionalRecord, tallyInput) {
  const claimById = new Map(tallyInput.findings.map(f => [f.id, f]));
  const byRaiser = {};
  const previousTier = {};
  for (const f of provisionalRecord.findings) {
    if (f.tier !== 'Contested' && f.tier !== 'Disputed') { continue; }
    previousTier[f.id] = f.tier;
    const src = claimById.get(f.id) || {};
    const peerVerdicts = (f.adjudications || []).filter(a => a.judge !== f.raiser).map(a => a.verdict);
    (byRaiser[f.raiser] = byRaiser[f.raiser] || []).push({
      id: f.id, claim: src.claim, severity: f.severity, peerVerdicts, disputeReasons: [],
    });
  }
  return { byRaiser, previousTier };
}

async function runDefenseSolo(ctx, raiser, findings, idx) {
  const brief = dbrief.buildDefenseBrief({ findings, date: ctx.o.date });
  const waveId = `${ctx.o.runId}-d${idx + 1}`;
  const expectedIds = findings.map(f => f.id);
  // Record the sub-wave BEFORE launching: `amicus abort` cascades over
  // stages[].waveIds (run-stages.js's record(), run.js's chair chain), so an id
  // written after the launch leaves an in-flight debate leg reachable only by
  // the pid kill. The v4.0.1 abort-cascade fix must hold for debate stages too.
  runState.appendStageWave(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate-defense', waveId);
  let res = await ctx.launchers.launchSolo({
    model: raiser, prompt: brief, project: ctx.scratchDir, waveId,
    timeout: ctx.o.timeout, gateway: ctx.o.gateway,
    noValidateModel: ctx.o.noValidateModel, noCostGate: ctx.o.noCostGate,
  });
  ctx.addWave(res.wave);
  if (isAbortExit(res.exitCode)) { return { raiser, aborted: res.exitCode }; }
  let leg = res.leg && res.leg.status === 'complete' ? res.leg : null;
  let parsed = leg ? parseDebateDefense(leg.summary, expectedIds) : { ok: false, byId: {}, errors: [{ code: 'DEAD_LEG', detail: 'no summary' }] };
  let conformance = leg ? 'clean' : 'unstructured';
  if (leg && !parsed.ok) {
    const repair = dbrief.buildDefenseRepairPrompt({ errors: parsed.errors });
    runState.appendStageWave(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate-defense', `${waveId}r`);
    const res2 = await ctx.launchers.launchSolo({ model: raiser, prompt: repair, project: ctx.scratchDir,
      waveId: `${waveId}r`, timeout: ctx.o.timeout, gateway: ctx.o.gateway,
      noValidateModel: ctx.o.noValidateModel, noCostGate: ctx.o.noCostGate });
    ctx.addWave(res2.wave);
    if (isAbortExit(res2.exitCode)) { return { raiser, aborted: res2.exitCode }; }
    const leg2 = res2.leg && res2.leg.status === 'complete' ? res2.leg : null;
    parsed = leg2 ? parseDebateDefense(leg2.summary, expectedIds) : parsed;
    conformance = parsed.ok ? 'repaired' : 'unstructured';
    if (leg2) { leg = leg2; }
  } else if (leg) {
    conformance = 'clean';
  }
  // Block-level failure after repair → every expected id no-response (parser already did this).
  // A dead leg (no complete summary) OR an 'unstructured' conformance after the one repair
  // is a debate degradation (spec §5.7) — surfaced via the returned leg for the exit-2 check.
  return { raiser, byId: parsed.byId, leg: leg ? { model: raiser, status: leg.status, durationMs: leg.durationMs, usage: leg.usage, conformance, summary: leg.summary } : { model: raiser, status: 'error', durationMs: null, usage: null, conformance: 'unstructured', summary: '' } };
}

async function runRevoteWave(ctx, judges, bundleFindings) {
  const bundle = dbrief.buildRevoteBundle({ findings: bundleFindings, date: ctx.o.date });
  // spec §5.1 names `revote-bundle.md` a run-dir artifact: the shared re-vote prompt goes
  // to disk exactly like Stage 2's bundle-stage2.md (run-stages.js:150), so the round's
  // model-facing input is auditable alongside briefing-stage1.md and chair-packet.md.
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ctx.o.runDir, 'revote-bundle.md'), bundle, { mode: 0o600 });
  const waveId = `${ctx.o.runId}-rv`;
  const expectedIds = bundleFindings.map(f => f.id);
  // The re-vote wave launches from inside runDebate, so run-debate — not run.js —
  // owns this stage's `running` checkpoint AND its abort-cascade id: only this
  // function knows whether the wave actually launched (it is skipped when nothing
  // was defended/amended or the cost ceiling hit).
  runState.updateStage(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate-revote',
    { status: 'running', startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), project: ctx.scratchDir, waveId });
  runState.appendStageWave(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate-revote', waveId);
  const res = await ctx.launchers.launchWave({
    models: judges, prompt: bundle, project: ctx.scratchDir, waveId,
    timeout: ctx.o.timeout, gateway: ctx.o.gateway,
    noValidateModel: ctx.o.noValidateModel, noCostGate: ctx.o.noCostGate,
  });
  ctx.addWave(res.wave);
  if (isAbortExit(res.exitCode)) { return { aborted: res.exitCode }; }
  const byJudge = {};
  const legs = [];
  for (const leg of (res.wave.legs || [])) {
    const alive = leg.status === 'complete' && leg.summary;
    let outLeg = leg;               // the leg actually recorded (post-repair when there is one)
    let parsed = alive ? parseRevote(leg.summary, expectedIds) : { ok: false, byId: {}, errors: [{ code: 'DEAD_LEG', detail: 'no summary' }] };
    let conformance = alive ? 'clean' : 'unstructured';
    if (alive && !parsed.ok) {
      // One repair, solo, to that judge.
      const repair = dbrief.buildRevoteRepairPrompt({ errors: parsed.errors });
      runState.appendStageWave(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate-revote', `${waveId}-${leg.model}r`);
      const r2 = await ctx.launchers.launchSolo({ model: leg.model, prompt: repair, project: ctx.scratchDir,
        waveId: `${waveId}-${leg.model}r`, timeout: ctx.o.timeout, gateway: ctx.o.gateway,
        noValidateModel: ctx.o.noValidateModel, noCostGate: ctx.o.noCostGate });
      ctx.addWave(r2.wave);
      if (isAbortExit(r2.exitCode)) { return { aborted: r2.exitCode }; }
      const leg2 = r2.leg && r2.leg.status === 'complete' ? r2.leg : null;
      parsed = leg2 ? parseRevote(leg2.summary, expectedIds) : parsed;
      conformance = parsed.ok ? 'repaired' : 'unstructured';
      // Symmetric with runDefenseSolo's `if (leg2) { leg = leg2; }` — otherwise
      // revote-<model>.md and the runStats row keep the PRE-repair output.
      if (leg2) { outLeg = leg2; }
    }
    byJudge[leg.model] = parsed.byId;
    legs.push({ model: leg.model, status: outLeg.status, durationMs: outLeg.durationMs, usage: outLeg.usage, conformance, summary: outLeg.summary || '' });
  }
  return { byJudge, legs };
}

/**
 * Full Stage-2.5 sequence (spec §5.1). Returns everything run.js needs.
 * Cost gate: the caller (run.js) already checks overBudget before invoking; this
 * function checks again before the re-vote wave (spec §5.7).
 */
async function runDebate(ctx, { provisionalRecord, tallyInput }) {
  const { byRaiser, previousTier } = debateTargets(provisionalRecord, tallyInput);
  const contested = provisionalRecord.findings.filter(f => f.tier === 'Contested').length;
  const disputed = provisionalRecord.findings.filter(f => f.tier === 'Disputed').length;

  // ---- Defense mini-wave: ONE CONCURRENT solo per raiser (spec §5.1) ----
  // Concurrent, not sequential: every raiser gets its OWN briefing, so this is N
  // independent solos rather than one fanout wave. There is no per-leg budget check to
  // interleave between them — the cost ceiling is a WHOLE-ROUND gate that run.js applies
  // BEFORE calling runDebate ('skipped-cost-ceiling' is a round-level outcome in spec
  // §5.1's enum, not a per-leg one). `runState.appendStageWave` is synchronous fs and each
  // solo registers its waveId before its first await, so concurrency cannot interleave a
  // read-modify-write of run.json.
  const raisers = Object.keys(byRaiser);
  const defenseResults = await Promise.all(
    raisers.map((raiser, i) => runDefenseSolo(ctx, raiser, byRaiser[raiser], i)));
  // A signal during the defense wave aborts the whole finalization (spec §5.7):
  // return the abort code so run.js finalizes 'aborted' with NO tally-final / NO ledger.
  const abortedDefense = defenseResults.find(d => d.aborted);
  if (abortedDefense) { return { aborted: abortedDefense.aborted, contested, disputed }; }
  materializeDebate(ctx.o.runDir, defenseResults.map(d => ({ model: d.raiser, summary: d.leg.summary })), 'rebuttal');

  // Attach previousTier to each defended finding for debate.json / decoration.
  const defenseByRaiser = {};
  for (const dr of defenseResults) {
    defenseByRaiser[dr.raiser] = {};
    for (const [id, resp] of Object.entries(dr.byId)) { defenseByRaiser[dr.raiser][id] = resp; }
  }
  // Stamp previousTier onto the tally input findings so applyDebate can read it.
  const stampedInput = { ...tallyInput, findings: tallyInput.findings.map(f => ({ ...f, previousTier: previousTier[f.id] })) };

  // Which findings were defended or amended (the re-vote bundle) — withdrawn/no-response never re-voted.
  const defendedOrAmended = [];
  for (const dr of defenseResults) {
    for (const [id, resp] of Object.entries(dr.byId)) {
      if (resp.action === 'defend' || resp.action === 'amend') {
        const src = tallyInput.findings.find(f => f.id === id) || {};
        defendedOrAmended.push({ id, severity: src.severity, amended: resp.action === 'amend',
          claim: resp.action === 'amend' ? resp.claim : src.claim, argument: resp.argument || 'defended without extra argument' });
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- Re-vote mini-wave (disputing judges only) ----
  let revoteByJudge = {};
  let revoteLegs = [];
  const bundledIds = defendedOrAmended.map(f => f.id);
  const judges = disputingJudges(provisionalRecord, bundledIds);
  // A re-vote is warranted only when something was defended/amended AND ≥1 judge disputed it.
  // Skipping THAT case because the whole-run budget is spent is the 'skipped-cost-ceiling'
  // degradation branch (spec §5.7); skipping because there is simply nothing to re-vote is NOT.
  const wouldRevote = defendedOrAmended.length > 0 && judges.length > 0;
  const costCeiling = ctx.overBudget() && wouldRevote;
  const skipRevote = ctx.overBudget() || !wouldRevote;
  if (!skipRevote) {
    const rv = await runRevoteWave(ctx, judges, defendedOrAmended);
    if (rv.aborted) { return { aborted: rv.aborted, contested, disputed }; }
    revoteByJudge = rv.byJudge;
    revoteLegs = rv.legs;
    // revote-<model>.md per surviving judge leg, mirroring rebuttal-<model>.md
    // (spec §5.1 'raw outputs revote-<model>.md'); Step 1's artifact test asserts it.
    materializeDebate(ctx.o.runDir, revoteLegs, 'revote');
  }

  // ---- Pure reassembly ----
  const { input: debatedInput, debateFindings } = applyDebate({ tallyInput: stampedInput, provisionalRecord, defenseByRaiser, revoteByJudge });
  debatedInput.runStats = [...(debatedInput.runStats || []),
    ...debateRunStatsRows({ defenseLegs: defenseResults.map(d => d.leg), revoteLegs })];

  // verdictChanges: findings whose tier moved from provisional to debated.
  const provTierById = new Map(provisionalRecord.findings.map(f => [f.id, f.tier]));
  const debatedRec = tally(debatedInput);
  let verdictChanges = 0;
  for (const f of debatedRec.findings) { if (provTierById.get(f.id) !== f.tier) { verdictChanges += 1; } }

  // ---- Artifacts + summary ----
  const revotesJson = [];
  for (const [judge, perId] of Object.entries(revoteByJudge)) {
    for (const [id, rv] of Object.entries(perId)) { revotesJson.push({ judge, id, verdict: rv.verdict, reason: rv.reason || null, applied: true }); }
  }
  const debateJson = { findings: debateFindings, revotes: revotesJson };
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ctx.o.runDir, 'debate.json'), JSON.stringify(debateJson, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });

  const counts = { defended: 0, amended: 0, withdrawn: 0, noResponse: 0 };
  for (const df of debateFindings) {
    if (df.action === 'defend') { counts.defended += 1; }
    else if (df.action === 'amend') { counts.amended += 1; }
    else if (df.action === 'withdraw') { counts.withdrawn += 1; }
    else { counts.noResponse += 1; }
  }
  const debateSummary = {
    enabled: true, outcome: costCeiling ? 'skipped-cost-ceiling' : 'ran',
    contested, disputed, ...counts,
    revoteJudges: revoteLegs.length, revoteApplied: revotesJson.length, verdictChanges,
  };

  // ---- Degradation (spec §5.7) → run.js maps this to exit code 2 ----
  // A dead/unstructured-after-repair defense solo, a partial or fully-dead re-vote wave,
  // or a cost-ceiling skip of a warranted re-vote each degrade the run. (Abort is handled
  // above by short-circuiting; nothing-to-debate and a clean run are NOT degradations.)
  const defenseDegraded = defenseResults.some(d => d.leg.status !== 'complete' || d.leg.conformance === 'unstructured');
  const revoteDegraded = revoteLegs.some(l => l.status !== 'complete' || l.conformance === 'unstructured');
  const degraded = defenseDegraded || revoteDegraded || costCeiling;

  // Chair-addendum outcomes (spec §5.3c).
  const priorById = new Map(provisionalRecord.findings.map(
    f => [f.id, Object.fromEntries((f.adjudications || []).map(a => [a.judge, a.verdict]))]));
  const addendumOutcomes = debateFindings.map(df => ({
    id: df.id, originalClaim: (tallyInput.findings.find(f => f.id === df.id) || {}).claim,
    action: require('./debate').PAST_TENSE[df.action], amendedClaim: df.action === 'amend' ? df.claim : null,
    priorVerdicts: priorById.get(df.id) || {},
    revotes: Object.fromEntries(revotesJson.filter(r => r.id === df.id).map(r => [r.judge, r.verdict])),
  }));

  return { debatedInput, debateFindings, debateSummary, addendumOutcomes,
    defenseLegs: defenseResults.map(d => d.leg), revoteLegs, verdictChanges,
    degraded, aborted: null };
}

module.exports = { runDebate, nothingToDebate, disputingJudges, debateTargets };
```

Add `materializeDebate` to `src/council/run-launch.js` (next to `materializeReviews`, reusing `sanitizeName`), and export it:

```js
/**
 * Write per-leg debate artifacts: `<prefix>-<sanitizeName(model)>.md` for each
 * leg with a non-empty summary. Mirrors materializeReviews.
 * @param {string} runDir @param {Array<{model, summary}>} legs @param {string} prefix
 */
function materializeDebate(runDir, legs, prefix) {
  const out = [];
  for (const leg of legs) {
    if (!leg || !leg.summary || !leg.summary.trim()) { continue; }
    const file = path.join(runDir, `${prefix}-${sanitizeName(leg.model)}.md`);
    fs.writeFileSync(file, leg.summary, { mode: 0o600 });
    out.push({ model: leg.model, file });
  }
  return out;
}
```

```js
module.exports = { createLaunchers, materializeReviews, materializeDebate, sanitizeName };
```

with the debate-aware version:

```js
    const provisionalInput = mkInput(null, o.chair);
    const provisional = tally(provisionalInput);

    // ---- Stage 2.5: debate (optional) ----
    let debatedProvisionalInput = provisionalInput;
    let debatedProvisional = provisional;
    let debateOutcomes = null;
    let debateSummary = o.debate ? { enabled: true, outcome: 'nothing-to-debate',
      contested: 0, disputed: 0, defended: 0, amended: 0, withdrawn: 0, noResponse: 0,
      revoteJudges: 0, revoteApplied: 0, verdictChanges: 0 } : null;
    if (o.debate) {
      // spec §5.1: the provisional tally is ALSO an audit artifact, not just a stage
      // checkpoint — no ledger append, written before any debate leg launches.
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(o.runDir, 'tally-provisional.json'),
        JSON.stringify(provisional, null, 2), { mode: 0o600 });
      runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'tally-provisional', { status: 'complete', startedAt: now(), completedAt: now() });
      if (!runDebateMod.nothingToDebate(provisional) && !overBudget()) {
        runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'debate-defense', { status: 'running', startedAt: now(), project: ctx.scratchDir });
        const dbg = await runDebateMod.runDebate(ctx, { provisionalRecord: provisional, tallyInput: provisionalInput });
        // A signal mid-debate aborts finalization: no tally-final, no ledger (spec §5.7).
        // Close the debate summary FIRST: the initRun seed carries only enabled+outcome
        // and the run.json contract requires a valid `outcome` whenever the key exists,
        // so an aborted round still leaves a valid object (the counts runDebate knew before it
        // short-circuited; nothing downstream was finalized).
        if (dbg.aborted) {
          runState.checkpoint(o.runDir, { debate: { ...debateSummary, outcome: 'ran',
            contested: dbg.contested, disputed: dbg.disputed } });
          return finalize(dbg.aborted);
        }
        runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'debate-defense', { status: 'complete', completedAt: now() });
        // run-debate owns this stage's `running`/`waveId`/`waveIds` checkpoint — it is the
        // only caller that knows whether the wave actually launched. Never advertise a
        // `-rv` id here: a skipped re-vote would leave the abort cascade chasing a wave
        // that never existed (the v4.0 lens `-s1` phantom, run.js's stage1 comment).
        runState.updateStage(o.runDir, 'debate-revote', { status: 'complete', completedAt: now() });
        debatedProvisionalInput = dbg.debatedInput;
        debatedProvisional = tally(dbg.debatedInput);
        debateOutcomes = dbg.addendumOutcomes;
        debateSummary = dbg.debateSummary;
        // Dead/unstructured defense, partial/fully-dead re-vote, or a cost-ceiling re-vote
        // skip degrade the run → exit 2 (spec §5.7), same channel as a v4.0 dead Stage-1 leg.
        if (dbg.degraded) { degraded.value = true; }
      } else if (overBudget() && !runDebateMod.nothingToDebate(provisional)) {
        // Budget exhausted before the defense wave even launched, but there WAS something to
        // debate — the other cost-ceiling branch (spec §5.7). Nothing debated; the run degrades
        // to exit 2. (A nothing-to-debate run that is also over budget stays 'nothing-to-debate'.)
        debateSummary.outcome = 'skipped-cost-ceiling';
        degraded.value = true;
      }
      runState.checkpoint(o.runDir, { debate: debateSummary });
    }

    const packet = stage2.buildChairPacket({
      reviews: s1.reviews.map(r => ({ model: r.model, text: r.text })),
      rankings: debatedProvisionalInput.rankings,
      adjudications: debatedProvisionalInput.adjudications,
      tierCounts: debatedProvisional.tierCounts,
      date: o.date,
    }) + (debateOutcomes ? '\n\n' + require('./briefings-debate').buildDebateAddendum({ outcomes: debateOutcomes }) : '');

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the v4.1 fake-launcher helper bodies** — append the block below to `tests/council/helpers/fake-launchers.js` (do NOT touch the v4.0 `review`/`judgeOut`/`mkLeg`/`okWave`/`scriptedLaunchers`/`happyScript`/`baseOptions` exports — Task 0 Step 2 verified them; `run-happy.test.js` still consumes `scriptedLaunchers(happyScript())` with runId `abc123`). These new helpers reuse the v4.0 leg/wave builders (`mkLeg`/`okWave`), are keyed to **runId `'r'`** (matching every v4.1 driver test's `runId: 'r'`), and each returns a launcher object `{launchWave, launchSolo, calls}` — so a test can pass the return value straight as `deps.launchers`:

```js
// ---- v4.1 debate + claude-review scripted launchers (append; runId 'r') ----

/** A defense-solo output body: prose + trailing {"responses":[…]} JSON block. */
const defenseOut = (responses) =>
  `Defending.\n\n\`\`\`json\n${JSON.stringify({ responses })}\n\`\`\`\n`;

/** A re-vote leg output body: prose + trailing {"revotes":[…]} JSON block. */
const revoteOut = (revotes) =>
  `Re-voting.\n\n\`\`\`json\n${JSON.stringify({ revotes })}\n\`\`\`\n`;

/**
 * Build launchers from a flat `{[waveId]: (opts) => {wave, exitCode}}` map (the
 * same shape scriptedLaunchers dispatches on), firing an optional onLaunch(opts)
 * hook exactly ONCE per launch (wave or solo). Throws on an unscripted waveId so
 * a test can prove a launch did NOT happen (e.g. no leg for 'claude'). Records
 * every launch's opts in `.calls`; solos expose `leg = wave.legs[0] || null`.
 */
function launchersFromScript(script, onLaunch) {
  const calls = [];
  async function dispatch(opts) {
    calls.push(opts);
    if (onLaunch) { onLaunch(opts); }
    const fn = script[opts.waveId];
    if (!fn) { throw new Error(`no script for waveId ${opts.waveId}`); }
    return fn(opts);
  }
  return {
    calls,
    launchWave: (opts) => dispatch(opts),
    launchSolo: async (opts) => {
      const r = await dispatch({ ...opts, models: [opts.model] });
      return { ...r, leg: (r.wave && r.wave.legs && r.wave.legs[0]) || null };
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Happy 3-bench script map keyed to runId 'r': gemini/gpt/qwen review (A1/B1/C1),
 * all three judge A1/B1/C1/D1, deepseek chairs with a VERDICT line. D1 covers a
 * Claude review added as label D (Task 7): the D1 adjudications are valid ONLY
 * BECAUSE a claude-review file is present. Without one, D1 is an unknown id —
 * `parse-stage2` pushes `UNKNOWN_FINDING_ID`, which makes `ok:false` and triggers
 * the bounded judge-repair loop whose repair waveId is not in this script, so
 * `launchersFromScript` throws. Use this map only with a claude review present.
 */
function happyScriptMap() {
  return {
    'r-s1': (opts) => okWave(opts.models.map(m => mkLeg(m, review(m)))),
    'r-s2': () => okWave([
      mkLeg('gemini', judgeOut(['Review B', 'Review C', 'Review A'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'neutral' }, { id: 'D1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
      mkLeg('gpt', judgeOut(['Review A', 'Review C', 'Review B'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'dispute' }, { id: 'D1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
      mkLeg('qwen', judgeOut(['Review A', 'Review B', 'Review C'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'neutral' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'D1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
    ]),
    'r-ch1': () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Synthesis of the bench.\n\nVERDICT: Ship it', 'complete', 0.03)]),
  };
}

/** Happy launchers keyed to runId 'r' (claude-review test). onLaunch counts launches. */
function happyLaunchers({ onLaunch } = {}) {
  return launchersFromScript(happyScriptMap(), onLaunch);
}

/**
 * Debate script map keyed to runId 'r'. A1 (raised by gemini) is Disputed by
 * peers gpt+qwen; gemini defends A1 (r-d1); the re-vote wave [gpt,qwen] (r-rv)
 * flips gpt→agree and holds qwen→dispute, moving A1 Disputed→Contested (one
 * verdict change); deepseek chairs (r-ch1). Override individual keys for the
 * degradation cases (dead defense, partial/dead re-vote, cost ceiling, abort).
 */
function debateScriptMap() {
  return {
    'r-s1': (opts) => okWave(opts.models.map(m => mkLeg(m, review(m)))),
    'r-s2': () => okWave([
      mkLeg('gemini', judgeOut(['Review B', 'Review C', 'Review A'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
      mkLeg('gpt', judgeOut(['Review B', 'Review C', 'Review A'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'dispute' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
      mkLeg('qwen', judgeOut(['Review B', 'Review C', 'Review A'],
        [{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'dispute' }, { id: 'B1', verdict: 'agree' }, { id: 'C1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
    ]),
    'r-d1': () => okWave([mkLeg('gemini', defenseOut([{ id: 'A1', action: 'defend', argument: 'the retry caps at 5' }]))]),
    'r-rv': () => okWave([
      mkLeg('gpt', revoteOut([{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree', reason: 'defense convincing' }])),
      mkLeg('qwen', revoteOut([{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'dispute', reason: 'still unsupported' }])),
    ]),
    'r-ch1': () => okWave([mkLeg('deepseek', 'Synthesis after debate.\n\nVERDICT: Fix these first', 'complete', 0.03)]),
  };
}

/** Debate launchers keyed to runId 'r' (happy debate path). onLaunch optional. */
function debateScript({ onLaunch } = {}) {
  return launchersFromScript(debateScriptMap(), onLaunch);
}
```

Extend the module.exports (keep every v4.0 key):

```js
module.exports = { review, judgeOut, mkLeg, okWave, scriptedLaunchers, happyScript, baseOptions,
  defenseOut, revoteOut, launchersFromScript,
  happyScriptMap, happyLaunchers, debateScriptMap, debateScript };

The happy case proves ledger-append-once + decoration; the degradation block covers every §5.7 exit-2 branch plus the abort and lenses-no-ledger cases §6 requires:

```js
const { runCouncil } = require('../../src/council/run');
const flh = require('./helpers/fake-launchers');
// revoteOut is already defined at module scope above (Step 1); reuse it. Pull the rest from the helper.
const { scriptedLaunchers, debateScriptMap, debateScript, okWave, mkLeg } = flh;

function e2eOpts(tmp, extra) {
  return { briefing: 'Review X', models: ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], chair: 'deepseek',
    project: tmp, runId: 'r', runDir: tmp, date: '2026-07-19', debate: true, ...extra };
}

describe('runCouncil --debate happy path (fake launchers)', () => {
  test('ledger appends exactly once; run.json carries the debate summary; tally.json decorated; exit 0', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'run-debate-e2e-'));
    let appends = 0;
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp),
      { launchers: debateScript(), appendRunFn: () => { appends += 1; } });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(appends).toBe(1);                          // provisional never appends; final appends once
    expect(run.debate.outcome).toBe('ran');
    expect(run.debate.verdictChanges).toBe(1);
    const tallyDoc = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'tally.json'), 'utf-8'));
    expect(tallyDoc.findings.some(f => f.debate)).toBe(true);
    // spec §5.1 audit artifact: the provisional tally is written, and is the
    // PRE-debate record (A1 still Disputed) — distinct from the decorated final tally.
    const provPath = path.join(tmp, 'tally-provisional.json');
    expect(fs.existsSync(provPath)).toBe(true);
    const prov = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(provPath, 'utf-8'));
    expect(prov.findings.find(f => f.id === 'A1').tier).toBe('Disputed');
    expect(prov.findings.some(f => f.debate)).toBe(false);
  });

  test('WITHOUT --debate run.json carries no `debate` key at all (durable contract)', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'run-debate-off-'));
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp, { debate: false }),
      { launchers: debateScript(), appendRunFn: () => {} });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect('debate' in run).toBe(false);   // a `debate:null` seed would fail Task 5's schema
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'tally-provisional.json'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('runCouncil --debate registers its sub-waves for the abort cascade', () => {
  // Same idiom as tests/council/run-waveids.test.js: read run.json from INSIDE the
  // launch, proving the id was written BEFORE the leg went in flight.
  test('debate-defense and debate-revote carry their in-flight waveIds', async () => {
    const runState = require('../../src/council/run-state');
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-waveids-'));
    const stageOf = (name) =>
      ((runState.readRun(tmp) || {}).stages || []).find(s => s.name === name) || null;
    let midDefense = null;
    let midRevote = null;
    const map = { ...debateScriptMap() };
    const d1 = map['r-d1'];
    const rv = map['r-rv'];
    map['r-d1'] = (opts) => { midDefense = stageOf('debate-defense'); return d1(opts); };
    map['r-rv'] = (opts) => { midRevote = stageOf('debate-revote'); return rv(opts); };
    const { exitCode } = await runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp),
      { launchers: scriptedLaunchers(map), appendRunFn: () => {} });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    // abortCouncilRun (src/mcp-council-awareness.js:161) cascades ONLY over stages that are
    // status 'running' AND carry a truthy `project` — waveIds alone would be unreachable.
    expect(midDefense.status).toBe('running');
    expect(midDefense.project).toBeTruthy();
    expect(midDefense.waveIds).toContain('r-d1');
    expect(midRevote.status).toBe('running');
    expect(midRevote.project).toBeTruthy();
    expect(midRevote.waveIds).toContain('r-rv');
  });

  test('a skipped re-vote never advertises the `-rv` wave id', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-norv-'));
    // gemini withdraws A1 → nothing defended/amended → the re-vote wave never launches.
    const map = { ...debateScriptMap(),
      'r-d1': () => okWave([mkLeg('gemini', defenseOut([{ id: 'A1', action: 'withdraw' }]))]) };
    delete map['r-rv'];                       // scriptedLaunchers throws if it launches anyway
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp),
      { launchers: scriptedLaunchers(map), appendRunFn: () => {} });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const rvStage = run.stages.find(s => s.name === 'debate-revote');
    expect(rvStage.waveId).toBeUndefined();
    expect(rvStage.waveIds).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('runCouncil --debate degradation → exit 2 (spec §5.7 / §6)', () => {
  // Each case overrides one key of the happy debate map; scriptedLaunchers throws on an
  // unscripted waveId, so a skipped wave is proven by its absence from launchers.calls.
  function runWith(tmp, overrides, extra) {
    const launchers = scriptedLaunchers({ ...debateScriptMap(), ...overrides });
    const p = runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp, extra), { launchers, appendRunFn: () => {} });
    return p.then((r) => ({ ...r, calls: launchers.calls }));
  }

  test('dead defense solo (no leg) → exit 2, outcome still "ran"', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-deaddef-'));
    const { exitCode, run } = await runWith(tmp, { 'r-d1': () => okWave([]) });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(run.debate.outcome).toBe('ran');
  });

  test('unstructured defense after the one repair → exit 2', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-unstrdef-'));
    const { exitCode } = await runWith(tmp, {
      'r-d1': () => okWave([mkLeg('gemini', 'prose only, no json block')]),
      'r-d1r': () => okWave([mkLeg('gemini', 'still no json block')]),
    });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
  });

  test('partial re-vote wave (one judge leg dead) → exit 2', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-partrv-'));
    const { exitCode } = await runWith(tmp, {
      'r-rv': () => okWave([
        mkLeg('gpt', revoteOut([{ id: 'A1', verdict: 'agree' }])),
        mkLeg('qwen', '', 'error'),
      ]),
    });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
  });

  test('fully-dead re-vote wave → exit 2', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-deadrv-'));
    const { exitCode } = await runWith(tmp, {
      'r-rv': () => okWave([mkLeg('gpt', '', 'error'), mkLeg('qwen', '', 'error')]),
    });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
  });

  test('cost ceiling BEFORE the defense wave → skipped-cost-ceiling, exit 2, no defense launched', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-cc-pre-'));
    // Make Stage 2 blow the whole-run ceiling so overBudget() is true at the debate gate.
    const dear = (m) => mkLeg(m, debateScriptMap()['r-s2']().wave.legs.find(l => l.model === m).summary, 'complete', 5);
    const { exitCode, run, calls } = await runWith(tmp,
      { 'r-s2': () => okWave(['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'].map(dear)) }, { maxCost: 1 });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(run.debate.outcome).toBe('skipped-cost-ceiling');
    expect(calls.every(c => c.waveId !== 'r-d1')).toBe(true);   // defense never launched
  });

  test('cost ceiling BEFORE the re-vote wave → skipped-cost-ceiling, exit 2, no re-vote launched', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-cc-rv-'));
    // Cheap stage1/2 (under the ceiling at the debate gate); an expensive defense leg
    // pushes spend past the ceiling before the re-vote wave is considered.
    const bigDefense = () => okWave([mkLeg('gemini',
      `Defending.\n\n\`\`\`json\n${JSON.stringify({ responses: [{ id: 'A1', action: 'defend', argument: 'x' }] })}\n\`\`\`\n`,
      'complete', 5)]);
    const { exitCode, run, calls } = await runWith(tmp, { 'r-d1': bigDefense }, { maxCost: 1 });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(run.debate.outcome).toBe('skipped-cost-ceiling');
    expect(calls.every(c => c.waveId !== 'r-rv')).toBe(true);   // re-vote never launched
  });

  test('abort mid-debate (defense wave signalled) → finalize aborted, NO tally-final, NO ledger', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-abort-'));
    let appends = 0;
    const launchers = scriptedLaunchers({ ...debateScriptMap(),
      'r-d1': () => okWave([], 143, 'aborted') });          // abort exit code, no leg
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(e2eOpts(tmp),
      { launchers, appendRunFn: () => { appends += 1; } });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(143);
    expect(run.status).toBe('aborted');
    expect(appends).toBe(0);                                 // ledger never appended
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'tally.json'))).toBe(false);  // no tally-final written
    // Even aborted, the debate summary honors the WRITER contract (Shared contracts: the
    // key always carries a valid outcome). Task 5's schema requires only `enabled`.
    expect(run.debate).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ enabled: true, outcome: 'ran' }));
  });

  test('lenses + debate → run.json carries the debate summary but NO ledger row', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'dbg-lenses-'));
    let appends = 0;
    // Under --lenses Stage 1 is one solo per seat: run-stages.js launches one
    // launchSolo per model with waveId `<runId>-l<i+1>` and run.js writes NO waveId
    // on the stage1 checkpoint (there is no `-s1` wave to name). Verified in Task 0.
    const map = { ...debateScriptMap() };
    delete map['r-s1'];
    map['r-l1'] = (opts) => okWave([mkLeg('gemini', flh.review('gemini'))]);
    map['r-l2'] = (opts) => okWave([mkLeg('gpt', flh.review('gpt'))]);
    map['r-l3'] = (opts) => okWave([mkLeg('qwen', flh.review('qwen'))]);
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(
      e2eOpts(tmp, { lenses: ['security architect', 'growth-stage VC', 'skeptical buyer'] }),
      { launchers: scriptedLaunchers(map), appendRunFn: () => { appends += 1; } });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(run.debate.outcome).toBe('ran');
    expect(appends).toBe(0);                                 // lens runs never feed the ledger (v4.0 gate)
    expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(tmp, 'tally.json'))).toBe(true);   // tally-final still written
  });
});
```

The happy `debateScript()` (Step 6) produces valid Stage-1 reviews, Stage-2 judge outputs disputing A1, gemini's defense of A1, the re-vote flip, and a chair `VERDICT:` line — modeled on the v4.0 happy-path script with the `r-d1`/`r-rv` keys added.

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ajv` (devDep, landed by v4.0 Plan A), the three council schemas (Plan A authored them).
- Produces: schemas that accept the additive debate/claude fields (and still reject a malformed one); `buildVerdict` copies `f.debate` when present. **Additive only — council family stays schemaVersion 2** (Global Constraints).

```js
// tests/council/run-schema-debate.test.js

const path = require('path');
const Ajv = require('ajv/dist/2020');

const SCHEMA_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'schemas');
const load = (name) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(SCHEMA_DIR, name), 'utf-8'));
const ajv = new Ajv({ allErrors: true, strict: false });

describe('council-run schema accepts the debate summary (spec §5.1)', () => {
  const validate = ajv.compile(load('council-run.schema.json'));
  const base = {
    schemaVersion: 2, type: 'council-run', runId: 'aaaa1111', status: 'complete',
    bench: ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], chair: 'deepseek', critic: null, lenses: null,
    labelMap: { 'Review A': 'gemini' }, options: { timeout: 10, maxCost: 2, gateway: 'auto', outDir: null },
    // The SHIPPED schema pins `usage` to `{"type":"object"}`, so `usage: null` is REJECTED.
    // A finalized run.json carries `usage: {cost}` (run.js `finalize`), not the `usage: null`
    // that `initRun` seeds — `null` here makes every positive case fail for the wrong reason.
    usage: { cost: { amount: 0.42, source: 'reported' } }, createdAt: '2026-07-19T00:00:00.000Z',
    stages: [
      { name: 'stage1', status: 'complete' }, { name: 'stage2', status: 'complete' },
      { name: 'tally-provisional', status: 'complete' }, { name: 'debate-defense', status: 'complete' },
      { name: 'debate-revote', status: 'complete' }, { name: 'tally-final', status: 'complete' },
      { name: 'chair', status: 'complete' }, { name: 'verdict', status: 'complete' },
    ],
    debate: { enabled: true, outcome: 'ran', contested: 1, disputed: 1, defended: 1, amended: 0,
      withdrawn: 1, noResponse: 0, revoteJudges: 2, revoteApplied: 2, verdictChanges: 1 },
  };
  test('valid debate run.json passes', () => {
    expect(validate(base)).toBe(true);
  });
  test('a non-debate run.json (no debate key) still passes', () => {
    // Task 4 writes the `debate` key ONLY when --debate is on, so `debate` must stay OUT of the
    // schema's top-level `required` (spec §5.1: without --debate there is no `debate` key).
    const { debate, ...noDebate } = base;
    expect(validate(noDebate)).toBe(true);
  });
  test('a partially-written debate object (only `enabled`) still passes', () => {
    // Task 4's initRun seed carries `outcome` too, but the schema deliberately requires only
    // `enabled`, so a bare-seed `debate` object left by any partial write still validates —
    // `outcome` must NOT be required (see Step 3).
    expect(validate({ ...base, status: 'aborted', exitCode: 143, debate: { enabled: true } })).toBe(true);
  });
  test('a bad debate.outcome enum is rejected', () => {
    expect(validate({ ...base, debate: { ...base.debate, outcome: 'maybe' } })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('council-tally / council-verdict accept findings[].debate (spec §5.6)', () => {

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**

Run: `npm test -- tests/council/run-schema-debate.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — but note WHICH cases go red. The v4.0 schemas are permissive (no `additionalProperties:false` on the run doc or on tally/verdict findings), so today an unknown `debate` blob is accepted *unchecked* and every positive-validation case passes from the start — those are regression pins, not the red bar. The red bar is `a bad debate.outcome enum is rejected` (ajv still returns `true`, because there is no `debate` sub-schema yet) and the `verdict.findings[0].debate` assertion (`buildVerdict` doesn't carry the decoration, so it is `undefined`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

In `schemas/council-run.schema.json`, add to `properties` (additive; do not set `additionalProperties:false` if it was already open — Plan A schemas are permissive):

```json
"debate": {
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "enabled": { "type": "boolean" },
    "outcome": { "enum": ["ran", "nothing-to-debate", "skipped-cost-ceiling"] },
    "contested": { "type": "integer" }, "disputed": { "type": "integer" },
    "defended": { "type": "integer" }, "amended": { "type": "integer" },
    "withdrawn": { "type": "integer" }, "noResponse": { "type": "integer" },
    "revoteJudges": { "type": "integer" }, "revoteApplied": { "type": "integer" },
    "verdictChanges": { "type": "integer" }
  },
  "required": ["enabled"],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

Only `enabled` is required: Task 4's `initRun` seed carries `outcome: 'nothing-to-debate'` from the first write, but the schema stays permissive on `outcome` so a bare-seed `debate` object left by any partial write still validates rather than turning a legitimate aborted run red. The `outcome` enum stays exactly the spec's three values (no `'aborted'` member — spec §5.1 is authoritative). Do **not** add `debate` to the schema's **top-level** `required` array either: non-debate runs omit the key entirely (spec §5.1: without `--debate` there is no `debate` key), which the second test case pins.

`stages[].name` is `{ "type": "string" }` in the shipped schema — a free string, not a closed enum — so `"tally-provisional"`, `"debate-defense"`, `"debate-revote"` and `"tally-final"` validate with no schema change.

In `schemas/council-tally.schema.json` and `schemas/council-verdict.schema.json`, add to each `findings[]` item's `properties`:

```json
"debate": {
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "action": { "enum": ["defended", "amended", "withdrawn", "no-response"] },
    "previousTier": { "type": ["string", "null"] }
  },
  "required": ["action"],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

In `src/council/verdict.js`, carry the field through inside `buildVerdict`'s `findings.map`. The v4.0/Plan-A return object builds each finding explicitly; add one line so a decorated tally finding surfaces on the verdict finding:

```js
    findings: record.findings.map(f => {
      const d = byId.get(f.id) || {};
      const tierOverride = d.tierOverride || f.tierOverride || null;
      const out = {
        id: f.id, raiser: f.raiser, severity: f.severity,
        tier: tierOverride ? tierOverride.to : f.tier,
        basis: f.basis, confidence: f.confidence, tierOverride,
        duplicateOf: d.duplicateOf || null,
        adjudications: f.adjudications,
        decision: d.decision || null,
        applied: d.applied === true,
      };
      if (f.debate) { out.debate = f.debate; }   // v4.1: additive debate decoration carry-through (spec §5.6)
      return out;
    }),
```

  const debate = {
    present: debated.length > 0,
    withdrawn: verdict.findings.filter(f => f.debate && f.debate.action === 'withdrawn')
      .map(f => ({ id: f.id, previousTier: f.debate.previousTier, tier: f.tier })),
    movements: verdict.findings.filter(f => f.debate && f.debate.previousTier && f.debate.previousTier !== f.tier)
      .map(f => ({ id: f.id, action: f.debate.action, previousTier: f.debate.previousTier, tier: f.tier })),
  };
```

(`findings` in `toModel` is the mapped view; `f.debate` must be preserved into it — add `debate: f.debate || null` to the object built in the `verdict.findings.map` inside `toModel`.) Add `debate` to the returned model object.

Add a render block in `renderMd(m)` after the "Findings by tier" section, before "## Cost":

```js
  if (m.debate.present) {
    out.push('\n## Debate round\n');
    if (m.debate.withdrawn.length) {
      out.push('**Withdrawn by raiser:**');
      for (const w of m.debate.withdrawn) { out.push(`- ${w.id} (was ${w.previousTier})`); }
      out.push('');
    }
    if (m.debate.movements.length) {
      out.push('**Tier movements after re-vote:**');
      for (const mv of m.debate.movements) { out.push(`- ${mv.id}: ${mv.previousTier} → ${mv.tier} (${mv.action})`); }
      out.push('');
    }
  }
```

In `src/council/report-html.js`, `renderHtml(m)` receives the same model (the parameter is named `m`, not `model`); add an equivalent conditional block — an `<h2>Debate round</h2>` heading plus a withdrawn `<ul>` and a movements `<ul>` (the file uses bare `<h2>` section headings, not `<section>` wrappers) — interpolated into the returned template literal just before the `<h2>Cost</h2>` block, guarded by `m.debate && m.debate.present`. The guard MUST tolerate a missing key: `tests/council/report.test.js` calls `renderHtml` directly with a hand-built model that has no `debate` property. Use the file's existing `esc()` helper (there is no `escapeHtml` in this file) on every value (deterministic ids/tiers only — no model prose — but keep the escaping discipline the file already uses).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `validateFindings` (`src/council/findings.js`, EXISTING); `assignLabels`/`toGlobalFindings` (C3); `buildErrorDoc`/`failJson` conventions (`error-doc.js`); `runStage2(ctx, {reviews, labels, globalFindings})` (`src/council/run-stages.js:143`, V4.0-NEW — this task widens it with `extraLabeled`); `pickFallbackChair` (`src/council/run-chair.js` — moved there by Task 0.5; it was `run.js:42` on v4.0.1 and is still re-exported from `./run`).

Invalid file OR `--chair claude` ⇒ `finalize(1, {code, message})` before any launch (zero spend). "Always fresh" is a skill-side rule the engine cannot verify. **Newly pinned signature:** `runStage2(ctx, {reviews, labels, globalFindings, extraLabeled = []})` — `extraLabeled` entries land in the judge BUNDLE only; the judge ROSTER stays `reviews.map(r => r.modelInput)` (`run-stages.js:152`), so claude is judged but never judges. `pickFallbackChair` also excludes the literal `'claude'`, so the `claude` ledger row this feature creates can never be promoted to chair on a later run (spec §4.4 "never chairs").

```js
// tests/council/run-claude-review.test.js
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { runCouncil, pickFallbackChair } = require('../../src/council/run');
const { happyLaunchers } = require('./helpers/fake-launchers');

function writeReview(dir, ok) {
  const p = path.join(dir, 'review-claude.md');
  const body = ok
    ? 'Claude review prose.\n```json\n{"overall":"t","findings":[{"id":1,"severity":"major","claim":"c","location":"l","rationale":"r"}]}\n```\n'
    : 'Claude review prose but no findings block at all.';
  fs.writeFileSync(p, body);
  return p;
}

function opts(tmp, extra) {
  return { briefing: 'Review X', models: ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], chair: 'deepseek',
    project: tmp, runId: 'r', runDir: tmp, date: '2026-07-19', ...extra };
}

// Repo idiom for every driver test (run-happy/run-chair/run-degrade): inject the
// ledger + stats + signal seams. Without appendRunFn the run appends a REAL
// 'claude' row to the developer's ~/.amicus/council-ledger.jsonl — the exact row
// that makes claude a promotable fallback chair (see the pickFallbackChair test).
const noSignals = () => () => {};

describe('--claude-review valid file', () => {
  test('claude joins the bundle as review N+1; meta + runStats reflect it; no extra leg', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'claude-rev-'));
    const reviewPath = writeReview(tmp, true);
    let launchCount = 0;
    const script = happyLaunchers({ onLaunch: () => { launchCount += 1; } });
    const { exitCode } = await runCouncil(opts(tmp, { claudeReviewFile: reviewPath }),
      { launchers: script, appendRunFn: jest.fn(), statsFn: () => [], installSignalAbortFn: noSignals });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const tallyDoc = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'tally.json'), 'utf-8'));
    expect(tallyDoc.meta.claudeInCouncil).toBe(true);
    expect(tallyDoc.meta.models).toContain('claude');
    const row = tallyDoc.runStats.find(r => r.model === 'claude');
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ role: 'claude', status: 'complete', durationMs: null, usage: null, conformance: 'clean' });
    // claude's findings entered the tally with a run-global label id (D-series for a 3-seat bench)
    expect(tallyDoc.findings.some(f => f.id.startsWith('D'))).toBe(true);
    // §4.4: the chair packet includes Claude's de-anonymized review like any other bench review.
    const packet = fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'chair-packet.md'), 'utf-8');
    expect(packet).toContain('Review by claude');
    expect(packet).toContain('Claude review prose.');
    // §4.4 BUNDLE vs ROSTER — claude's review IS in the Stage-2 judge bundle (label D)…
    const bundle = fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'bundle-stage2.md'), 'utf-8');
    expect(bundle).toContain('--- Review D ---');
    expect(bundle).toContain('Claude review prose.');
    expect(bundle).toContain('D1 [major] c');   // its findings joined the run-global index
    // …but the judge ROSTER is bench-only: no judge leg is launched for claude.
    const s2call = script.calls.find(c => c.waveId === 'r-s2');
    expect(s2call.models).toEqual(['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen']);
    expect(script.calls.every(c => !(c.models || []).includes('claude'))).toBe(true);
    // exactly one launch per bench seat + one judge wave + one chair solo — none for 'claude'.
    expect(launchCount).toBe(3); // r-s1 (wave), r-s2 (wave), r-ch1 (solo); claude never launches
  });
});

describe('claude is never a fallback chair (spec §4.4 "never chairs")', () => {
  test('pickFallbackChair skips a claude ledger row even with the best street cred', () => {
    // Every claude-review run puts 'claude' in meta.models → the ledger → `council
    // stats`. pickFallbackChair only filters the bench and the failed chair, so
    // without an explicit exclusion a LATER run would promote claude to chair.
    const rows = [
      { model: 'claude', avgStreetCredPeersOnly: 1.0 },   // best (lowest mean rank)
      { model: 'kimi', avgStreetCredPeersOnly: 2.0 },
    ];
    expect(pickFallbackChair(rows, ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], 'deepseek')).toBe('kimi');
  });
});

describe('--claude-review invalid file → pre-flight error, zero spend', () => {
  test('exit 1, COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID, no launches', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'claude-bad-'));
    const reviewPath = writeReview(tmp, false);
    let launches = 0;
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(opts(tmp, { claudeReviewFile: reviewPath }),
      { launchers: { launchWave: async () => { launches += 1; return { wave: { legs: [] } }; },
        launchSolo: async () => { launches += 1; return { wave: { legs: [] }, leg: null }; } },
        appendRunFn: jest.fn(), statsFn: () => [], installSignalAbortFn: noSignals });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(1);
    expect(launches).toBe(0);
    expect(run.error.code).toBe('COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID');
    expect(run.error.message).toContain('council_claude_review_invalid');
    // The pre-flight runs AFTER initRun, so the error doc lands in a run dir that
    // exists — status 'error', exit 1, and no ledger row (nothing was tallied).
    const runDoc = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'run.json'), 'utf-8'));
    expect(runDoc.status).toBe('error');
  });
});

describe('--chair claude → pre-flight error', () => {
  test('exit 1, no launches', async () => {
    const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'chair-claude-'));
    const reviewPath = writeReview(tmp, true);
    let launches = 0;
    const { exitCode, run } = await runCouncil(opts(tmp, { chair: 'claude', claudeReviewFile: reviewPath }),
      { launchers: { launchWave: async () => { launches += 1; return { wave: { legs: [] } }; },
        launchSolo: async () => { launches += 1; return { wave: { legs: [] }, leg: null }; } },
        appendRunFn: jest.fn(), statsFn: () => [], installSignalAbortFn: noSignals });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(1);
    expect(launches).toBe(0);
    expect(run.error.code).toBe('COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID');
  });
});
```

(`happyLaunchers` is the shared v4.1 fake-launcher helper written in Task 4 Step 6 — it returns a launcher object keyed to runId `'r'` and takes an `onLaunch` hook so the test can assert no extra leg is launched for claude. `scriptedLaunchers` throws on any unscripted waveId, so a stray `claude` launch would fail the run outright.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

Add the error code to `src/utils/error-doc.js` `ERROR_CODES` (additive):

```js
  COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID: 'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID',
```

In `src/council/run.js`, add the pre-flight + injection. Near the top requires:

```js
const { validateFindings } = require('./findings');
```

Pre-flight goes **after** `runState.initRun` + `runState.writePointer` and after the `finalize` closure is defined (run.js:92-103) — i.e. immediately before `const ctx = { o, launchers, addWave, overBudget, scratchDir: … };` (run.js:105) — and before anything launches. It must NOT go before `initRun`: `initRun` is what `mkdirSync`s `o.runDir` (`run-state.js:79`), so an error doc written earlier would `ENOENT` into a directory that does not exist yet. Placed here the guard needs no new helper — it returns through the existing `finalize(1, …)`, which checkpoints `status:'error'`, `exitCode:1`, `error`, and a `usage.cost` summed over an empty `allLegs` (zero launches, zero spend):

```js
  // v4.1 §4.4: Claude-in-council is a file input, validated before any spend.
  let claudeReview = null;
  if (o.claudeReviewFile) {
    if (o.chair === 'claude') {
      return finalize(1, { code: 'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID',
        message: 'council_claude_review_invalid: claude may not chair (it is judged, never votes or chairs)' });
    }
    let text = '';
    try { text = fs.readFileSync(o.claudeReviewFile, 'utf-8'); }
    catch (e) {
      return finalize(1, { code: 'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID',
        message: `council_claude_review_invalid: cannot read ${o.claudeReviewFile}: ${e.message}` });
    }
    const v = validateFindings(text);
    if (!v.ok) {
      return finalize(1, { code: 'COUNCIL_CLAUDE_REVIEW_INVALID',
        message: `council_claude_review_invalid: ${v.errors.map(e => `${e.code}: ${e.detail}`).join('; ')}` });
    }
    claudeReview = { model: 'claude', text, findings: v.findings };
  }
```

No new helper is added: `finalize` (run.js:92) already uninstalls the signal handlers installed at run.js:84, computes `status` from the exit code (`1 → 'error'`), and checkpoints `error` + `usage`. Over an empty `allLegs`, `sumWaveUsage([])` yields `cost.amount === null` — nothing was launched, so nothing is invented. Exit is 1 with zero launches; the error doc lands in `<runDir>/run.json` where `amicus council show`/`status` already look for it.

Where the driver assembles Stage-2 labels and the tally input, thread the claude review as review N+1. Merged v4.0 `run.js:144-154` attaches each review's run-global ids ONTO the review object (`buildTallyInput` reads `r.globalFindings`, not a bare parallel array) and then hands `reviews`/`labels`/`globalFindings` to `runStage2`. Extend all three when `claudeReview` is present:

```js
    const reviewModels = s1.reviews.map(r => r.model);
    if (claudeReview) { reviewModels.push('claude'); }   // claude is ALWAYS the last label
    const labels = assignLabels(reviewModels);
    runState.checkpoint(o.runDir, { labelMap: labels.labelMap });
    s1.reviews.forEach((r, i) => {
      r.globalFindings = toGlobalFindings(labels.entries[i].letter, r.model, r.findings);
    });
    if (claudeReview) {
      const ci = labels.entries.length - 1;
      claudeReview.label = labels.entries[ci].label;     // e.g. 'Review D' on a 3-seat bench
      claudeReview.globalFindings =
        toGlobalFindings(labels.entries[ci].letter, 'claude', claudeReview.findings);
    }
    const globalFindings = s1.reviews.flatMap(r => r.globalFindings)
      .concat(claudeReview ? claudeReview.globalFindings : []);
```

**The judge BUNDLE must carry claude; the judge ROSTER must not (BLOCKER).** The Stage-2 bundle is not built in `run.js` — it is built in `src/council/run-stages.js`, which zips labels to reviews POSITIONALLY (`run-stages.js:148`) and derives the judge roster from the SAME array (`run-stages.js:152`, the only `buildJudgeBundle` call site in the codebase):

```js
  const labeled = labels.entries.map((e, i) => ({ label: e.label, text: reviews[i].text }));
  const bundle = stage2.buildJudgeBundle({ reviews: labeled, findings: globalFindings });
  …
  const judges = reviews.map(r => r.modelInput);
```

Pushing claude into `reviews` would therefore spawn a judge leg for claude (spec §4.4 forbids it), and leaving `labels` one entry longer than `reviews` would make the positional zip read `reviews[N].text` of `undefined`. Decouple them — widen `runStage2` with an `extraLabeled` array that lands in `labeled` only:

```js
/**
 * Stage 2: shared anonymized bundle → judge wave in _scratch → parse + repair.
 * @param {object} ctx
 * @param {{reviews: Array, labels: {entries, labelMap}, globalFindings: Array,
 *   extraLabeled?: Array<{label: string, text: string}>}} args
 *   `extraLabeled` (v4.1 §4.4) are labeled reviews sourced from a FILE rather than
 *   a leg (the Claude review): they join the judged bundle, never the judge roster.
 * @returns {Promise<{aborted: number|null, judgeResults: Array}>}
 */
async function runStage2(ctx, { reviews, labels, globalFindings, extraLabeled = [] }) {
```

and rebuild `labeled` off `reviews` (so the zip can never run past the end of the array) with the extras appended:

```js
  const labeled = reviews
    .map((r, i) => ({ label: labels.entries[i].label, text: r.text }))
    .concat(extraLabeled);
```

Leave `const judges = reviews.map(r => r.modelInput);` **untouched** — that one line is what keeps claude out of the judge wave. `parseCtx.labels` already derives from `labels.entries`, so judges may legally rank `Review D`, and `rankingToOrder` maps it back to `claude` through `labels.labelMap`.

Then pass the extra at the `run.js` call site (`run.js:154`):

```js
    const s2 = await runStage2(ctx, {
      reviews: s1.reviews, labels, globalFindings,
      extraLabeled: claudeReview ? [{ label: claudeReview.label, text: claudeReview.text }] : [],
    });
```

`extraLabeled` defaults to `[]`, so every v4.0 `runStage2` call site and every existing `run-stages` test is unaffected.

**Chair packet includes Claude's review (spec §4.4 — the load-bearing fix).** §4.4 requires "The chair packet includes the de-anonymized Claude review like any other." Task 4 Step 5 built the chair-packet `reviews` array from `s1.reviews.map(r => ({model, text}))` — which never contains `claude` (no leg is launched for it). Change **that** call site (the `stage2.buildChairPacket({ reviews: … })` introduced in Task 4 Step 5) to append Claude's entry:

```js
    const chairReviews = s1.reviews.map(r => ({ model: r.model, text: r.text }));
    if (claudeReview) { chairReviews.push({ model: 'claude', text: claudeReview.text }); }
    const packet = stage2.buildChairPacket({
      reviews: chairReviews,                       // ← was s1.reviews.map(...); now includes claude
      rankings: debatedProvisionalInput.rankings,
      adjudications: debatedProvisionalInput.adjudications,
      tierCounts: debatedProvisional.tierCounts,
      date: o.date,
    }) + (debateOutcomes ? '\n\n' + require('./briefings-debate').buildDebateAddendum({ outcomes: debateOutcomes }) : '');
```

Claude casts no rankings and no adjudications, so it surfaces only as one extra `--- Review by claude ---` block in the packet (spec §4.4), never in the rankings/adjudications sections. `buildChairPacket` already de-anonymizes with `Review by ${r.model}`, so `claude` is named like every bench reviewer.

Then pass the claude review through into `buildTallyInput` so `meta.models` includes `'claude'` and its findings reach the tally, plus a synthesized runStats row. Concretely, when building the tally input (`mkInput` / `asm.buildTallyInput`), add `'claude'` to the models list, concat `claudeReview.globalFindings` onto the findings (`buildTallyInput`'s `findings` comes from `reviews.flatMap(r => r.globalFindings)`, and claude is not in `reviews`), and append the runStats row:

```js
    const claudeStats = claudeReview
      ? { model: 'claude', role: 'claude', wasChair: false, conformance: 'clean',
          status: 'complete', durationMs: null, usage: null }
      : null;
```

Feed `claudeReview` + `claudeStats` into `asm.buildTallyInput` (extend that helper's args in `run-assemble.js` with `claudeReview?`/`claudeStats?`; when present it appends `'claude'` to `meta.models`, sets `meta.claudeInCouncil = true`, and pushes the runStats row). Claude casts NO rankings and is NEVER launched as a judge leg, so `computeStreetCred` naturally yields `withSelf == peersOnly` for it (the "How Claude's review fared" readout falls out of the normal artifacts). No judge leg is launched for `'claude'` because the judge wave models come from `s1.reviews` (bench), which never includes claude.

`meta.claudeInCouncil` default stays `false` (v4.0 pin) unless `claudeReview` is present — this is the amendment to the v4.0 meta pin (spec §4.5a).

**Claude must never become a fallback chair (spec §4.4).** Appending `'claude'` to `meta.models` puts a `claude` row in the council ledger (`ledger.js` `buildLedgerRows` maps over `meta.models`), so `deriveReliability()` returns a `claude` row with a real `avgStreetCredPeersOnly` — and `pickFallbackChair` — moved by Task 0.5 into `src/council/run-chair.js` (it was `run.js:42-49` on v4.0.1) — filters out only the bench and the failed chair. On a LATER run, where `claude` is not on the bench, a well-rated `claude` row would be promoted to chair, walking straight past the pre-flight `--chair claude` guard. Close it in the same filter, **editing `src/council/run-chair.js`**:

```js
  const candidates = (statsRows || [])
    .filter(r => r.model !== 'claude'                  // v4.1 §4.4: judged, never chairs
      && !benchSet.has(r.model) && r.model !== failedChair
      && typeof r.avgStreetCredPeersOnly === 'number')
    .sort((a, b) => a.avgStreetCredPeersOnly - b.avgStreetCredPeersOnly);
```

## Phase 3 — Flag surface + verdict render (CLI + MCP)

### Task 8: `--debate` / `--claude-review` / `--no-cost-gate` on `council run` (CLI + MCP)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/cli.js` (grandfathered — `isBooleanFlag` += `debate`; usage lines for `council run`)
- Modify: `src/cli-handlers-council-run.js` (V4.0-NEW — parse the three flags, forward as `debate`/`claudeReviewFile`/`noCostGate` options)
- Modify: `src/council/run-launch.js` (V4.0-NEW — `launchWave` forwards a per-call `noCostGate` into its `runFanout` call)
- Modify: `src/council/run-stages.js` (V4.0-NEW — the Stage-1/Stage-2 launch option objects carry `noCostGate`)
- Modify: `src/council/run-chair.js` (created by Task 0.5 — the chair attempts + VERDICT-line repair solo carry `noCostGate`)
- Modify: `src/mcp-council-run.js` (V4.0-NEW — accept `debate`/`claudeReviewFile`/`noCostGate` inputs and push them onto the spawned `council run` argv)
- Modify: `src/mcp-tools.js` (grandfathered — `amicus_council_run` inputSchema += the three inputs)
- Test: `tests/cli-council-run-flags.test.js`, `tests/mcp-council-run-inputs.test.js`, `tests/council/run-no-cost-gate.test.js` (Create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `runCouncil` options (C1 + Tasks 4/7); the v4.0 CLI handler + MCP handler.
- Produces: `amicus council run … --debate` → `runCouncil({debate:true})`; `--claude-review <path>` → `{claudeReviewFile: <abs>}`; `--no-cost-gate` → `{noCostGate:true}` forwarded to every internal launch (so the skill's "user asked for o3 by name" path is a single flag on the run, not one flag per repair/chair). MCP `amicus_council_run {debate?, claudeReviewFile?, noCostGate?}` maps identically — but the MCP handler SPAWNS a `council run` child instead of calling `runCouncil`, so its half of the mapping lands as `--debate` / `--claude-review <abs>` / `--no-cost-gate` on the spawned argv. Tool count stays 15; `--max-cost` still works alongside (they are independent: `--max-cost` is the whole-run ceiling, `--no-cost-gate` disables the per-leg price-threshold gate).

Create `tests/cli-council-run-flags.test.js`:

```js
// tests/cli-council-run-flags.test.js
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');

jest.mock('../src/council/run', () => ({ runCouncil: jest.fn() }));
const { runCouncil } = require('../src/council/run');
const { parseArgs } = require('../src/cli');

function briefingFile() {
  const p = path.join(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'brief-')), 'b.md');
  fs.writeFileSync(p, '# briefing');
  return p;
}

beforeEach(() => { runCouncil.mockReset(); runCouncil.mockResolvedValue({ exitCode: 0, run: { runId: 'r', status: 'complete' } }); });

// RED-PHASE NOTE: an UNREGISTERED `--flag` that sits at the end of argv (or in
// front of another `--flag`) already parses as `true` today (src/cli.js parseArgs
// falls through to `result[key] = true` when `next` starts with `--` or is
// missing) — a test shaped that way can never fail. The contract that actually
// needs guarding is: the parsed value is a BOOLEAN, and the following token is
// NOT consumed as the flag's value. Both assertions below fail before
// `isBooleanFlag` learns the flag.
test('--debate is a boolean flag and does not swallow the next token', () => {
  const args = parseArgs(['council', 'run', '--debate', 'extra']);
  expect(args.debate).toBe(true);          // before: 'extra' (string)
  expect(args._).toContain('extra');       // before: eaten as --debate's value
});

test('--render is a boolean flag and does not swallow the tally path', () => {
  const args = parseArgs(['council', 'verdict', '--render', 'tally.json']);
  expect(args.render).toBe(true);          // before: 'tally.json' (string)
  expect(args._).toEqual(['council', 'verdict', 'tally.json']);
});

test('council run forwards debate/claudeReviewFile/noCostGate to runCouncil', async () => {
  const { handleCouncil } = require('../src/cli-handlers-council');
  const bf = briefingFile();
  const rf = briefingFile();
  await handleCouncil({ _: ['council', 'run'], models: 'gemini,gpt,qwen', chair: 'deepseek',
    'prompt-file': bf, 'claude-review': rf, debate: true, 'no-cost-gate': true, json: true });
  expect(runCouncil).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  const opts = runCouncil.mock.calls[0][0];
  expect(opts.debate).toBe(true);
  expect(opts.noCostGate).toBe(true);
  expect(path.isAbsolute(opts.claudeReviewFile)).toBe(true);
});
```

Create `tests/mcp-council-run-inputs.test.js` (argv is the real boundary — `handleCouncilRunTool` never calls `runCouncil`; it validates, pre-seeds the run dir, then hands an argv to the injected `spawnFn`, so a `runCouncil` mock would never be touched and every assertion behind an `if` would silently skip). Idiom follows the existing `tests/mcp-council-run.test.js`:

```js
// tests/mcp-council-run-inputs.test.js
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { handleCouncilRunTool } = require('../src/mcp-council-run');

let tmp; let briefingFile; let reviewFile;
beforeEach(() => {
  tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-council-inputs-'));
  briefingFile = path.join(tmp, 'briefing.md');
  fs.writeFileSync(briefingFile, 'Review this.');
  reviewFile = path.join(tmp, 'review-claude.md');
  fs.writeFileSync(reviewFile, 'Claude prose.\n');
});
afterEach(() => { fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

// spawnFn is injected by mcp-server; capture the argv instead of spawning.
const helpers = (spawnCalls) => ({
  spawnFn: (args, dir) => { spawnCalls.push({ args, dir }); return { pid: 4242 }; },
  clientName: 'claude-code',
});
// `models` is an ARRAY on this tool (resolveBenchInput ignores a comma string).
const input = (extra = {}) => ({ briefingFile, models: ['gemini', 'gpt', 'qwen'], ...extra });

test('debate/claudeReviewFile/noCostGate reach the spawned council run argv', async () => {
  const calls = [];
  const res = await handleCouncilRunTool(
    input({ debate: true, claudeReviewFile: reviewFile, noCostGate: true }), tmp, helpers(calls));
  expect(res.isError).toBeUndefined();
  expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
  const args = calls[0].args;
  expect(args).toContain('--debate');
  expect(args).toContain('--no-cost-gate');
  expect(args[args.indexOf('--claude-review') + 1]).toBe(reviewFile);   // absolute
});

test('omitted inputs add no flags (v4.0 argv byte-unchanged)', async () => {
  const calls = [];
  await handleCouncilRunTool(input(), tmp, helpers(calls));
  const args = calls[0].args;
  expect(args).not.toContain('--debate');
  expect(args).not.toContain('--no-cost-gate');
  expect(args).not.toContain('--claude-review');
});
```

Create `tests/council/run-no-cost-gate.test.js` (the flag is worthless unless it survives all the way to `runFanout`; `scriptedLaunchers` records every launch's opts in `.calls`, which is the guard against a missed call site):

```js
// tests/council/run-no-cost-gate.test.js
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { runCouncil } = require('../../src/council/run');
const { createLaunchers } = require('../../src/council/run-launch');
const { scriptedLaunchers, happyScript, baseOptions } = require('./helpers/fake-launchers');

let tmp;
beforeEach(() => { tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'council-gate-')); });
afterEach(() => { fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

const noSignals = () => () => {};
const deps = (launchers) => ({
  launchers, appendRunFn: () => {}, statsFn: () => [], installSignalAbortFn: noSignals,
});

test('launchWave forwards noCostGate to runFanout', async () => {
  const seen = [];
  const fanoutFn = async (opts) => { seen.push(opts); return { wave: { status: 'complete', legs: [] }, exitCode: 0 }; };
  const { launchWave } = createLaunchers({ fanoutFn });
  await launchWave({ models: ['gemini'], prompt: 'p', project: tmp, waveId: 'w1', noCostGate: true });
  expect(seen[0].noCostGate).toBe(true);
});

test('noCostGate reaches EVERY internal launch (stage-1 wave, stage-2 wave, chair solo)', async () => {
  const launchers = scriptedLaunchers(happyScript());
  const { exitCode } = await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp, { noCostGate: true }), deps(launchers));
  expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
  expect(launchers.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  for (const call of launchers.calls) { expect(call.noCostGate).toBe(true); }
});

test('without the flag no launch claims it (the budget gate still runs)', async () => {
  const launchers = scriptedLaunchers(happyScript());
  await runCouncil(baseOptions(tmp), deps(launchers));
  for (const call of launchers.calls) { expect(call.noCostGate).toBeFalsy(); }
});
```
